# Looks audit 47, 2026-11-03 (round 88 merge: lanes x Windows, y Cupertino, z Material on master a6e39be41, tree merge88)

Independent check of the merged tree `merge88` with `neper-self.exe` of the Neper build. Own scratch comparers (python, numpy, PIL, not committed): flat pixel compares against the committed bytes, contrast probes at the pixel, glyph coverage measurement of the star, rule and checkbox geometry against `ref/flutter/material/*/datatable-rest@1x`, effective-status printing of the overlays in sorted order. Overlay `status.d/~audit47.json` (the character `~` sorts after the lane z file of 250 z; `ls | sort | tail` and Python `sorted` both put it last).

Result. 1 row downgraded (2 look entries: Cupertino table, Light and Dark), 1 row note added (Windows Dark dialog, stays review). Completion after regeneration, Light / Dark (the 30 Delphi-equivalent controls): Windows 99 / 99, Linux 100 / 100, Neper 100 / 100, macOS / iOS 100 / 100, Android 100 / 100.

(1) Merge sanity. The merged tree compiles. `surf_table` renders byte for byte as committed. Viewed after the conflict resolution: Material (widths 200 / 76, checkbox column, selection bar, sorted cell) is as intended; Cupertino (widths 56 / 104, "Creature" whole) is NOT: in the sorted descending cell the arrow is drawn on top of the "Qty" caption (56 wide), 1x and 1.5x, light and dark. The y lane recorded the overlap as a gap and still raised the row; a visible overlap is a defect, so table Cupertino and Cupertino Dark are back to review. 36 sheet apps were rendered one at a time (every app of the three lanes: data_grid, dialog, avatar, calendar, cup_date_edit, cup_group_box, cup_image_combo_box, date_range_picker, nav_toolbar, nav_bottom_app_bar, nav_sidebar, nav_navigation_drawer, nav_navigation_stack, surf_overlay, surf_table, color_picker, f_ribbon, duration_picker, notification_list, h_carousel, h_reorderable_list, plus button, checkbox, text_edit, tree_list, scroll_bar, memo, label, combo_box, status_bar, progress_bar, data_grid_dialogs, data_grid_dialog_states, dialog_native, dialog_message, data_grid_top, data_grid_bottom), `git status` read after each. Stale committed pictures found (restored, not committed): Windows Dark data_grid_main and data_grid_main@1.5x (22 and 48 pixels, at most 39 levels, all at the favourite star's edge fringe; lane x claimed it refreshed). Everything else, including every Neper, Neper Dark, Linux and Linux Dark picture of the 36 apps (well over 40 sheets), is byte for byte as committed. The four pictures lane x listed: Windows data_grid_main, Dark data_grid_dialogs and Dark native_dark4 are now exact; Dark data_grid_main is not.

(2) Effective status. Every overlay row of the three lanes printed in sorted order (x88, y88, the 250 z file): 45 look entries differ from master, all as intended (Windows Dark data_grid ready; 11 Cupertino idiom rows and avatar, image_combo_box, date_edit, group_box ready; Material duration_picker, notification_list, carousel, reorderable_list, ribbon, color_picker ready, table matched). No overlay of one lane overrides another's.

(3) Windows. data_grid Dark held. Star measured over the whole glyph with a coverage model (ink #EAEAEA on the #2B2B2B face): at 16 percent coverage or more the committed picture has 109 pixels (1x) and 238 (1.5x), mean 6.2:1, minimum 2.9:1 at the faintest fringe pixels; the fresh render has 123 and 268 pixels, minimum 3.39:1, mean 5.9:1; at 50 percent coverage and more both are 6.7:1 or better. The body is legible at both scales (viewed); held ready. dialog stays review: Deck Properties, About (inactive and active), Filter Card Pool, Advanced Filter, Conditional Formatting, Deck Legality, Customization and Statistics are all legible at 1x in Light and Dark and the Dark mapping holds (text lifted, chrome mapped, artwork raw), but in Dark the Missing Set Cards edition names keep coloured inks at 2.3 to 2.9:1 (AA5555, 5555AA); noted in the row.

(4) Cupertino viewed at 1x and 1.5x, Light and Dark. Held: image_combo_box (lit row white on 0A60FF; legible; Hover cell equals Rest, 0 px, as macOS pop-up buttons do), date_edit (chosen 5.1 and 7.0:1; limit stated), group_box (the long caption is whole, invalid red 4.8 and 5.0:1), avatar (the badge hangs off the corner; it grazes 3 to 9 pixels of the N foot's edge of 171 to 374 ink pixels, the monogram reads), calendar (chosen 5.08 and 6.95:1, range digits 6.1 and 6.5:1), toolbar, bottom_app_bar, title_bar (hovered close glyph), tree_table, popover, side_sheet, date_range_picker (and its open sheet), workspace (hint text 5.6 and 7.6:1, placeholder 6.0 and 4.5:1), key_value_editor. Header caption of table 4.5:1 (6E6E74 on F2F2F7). Downgraded: table.

(5) Material. table matched held: rule pitch 48 and header 56 as the reference, rule colour 202,196,208 against 202,196,207 (dark 73,69,79 against 73,69,78), ground equal, checkbox 18 px with its top 16 px under the rule against 15 in the reference (1 px), header caption centre 1 px off; the two recorded deviations (16 dp margin against 24, Type left aligned) are real. duration_picker Hover now draws the layer on the Minutes plus button (light and dark, viewed). notification_list (330 high, third notice and Details whole), carousel (Next and Previous layers), reorderable_list (24 dp handle, row layers), ribbon (glyphs drawn: clipboard, scissors, pages, B, I, U, table, picture; the contextual tab label legible) and color_picker (R, G, B, A letters and readings) all held at 1x, light and dark.

Honest limits. Contrasts are nominal ink pairs at the pixel; the 1.5x pictures of the Cupertino idiom rows were viewed for toolbar, bottom_app_bar, title_bar, workspace (Dark), popover, key_value_editor, date_range_picker_open, table and avatar, and by measurement for the rest; the Material pictures were viewed at 1x only (the 1.5x files render byte for byte as committed and were not opened), so the Material 1.5x legibility is the lane's claim.

'data_grid_main is the old desk layout' is honest: the 1:1 README records that the desk copy drifted and the main window cannot be reproduced; both its grids are the matched sheets, and the orange 3.0:1, green 4.47:1 and grey 3.2:1 row colours are v3's own (the same pixel colours occur in the data_grid_bottom capture). Other Dark ink holds (names 5.2 to 11:1, counter 14:1, group 12:1). dialog stays review: Options, Sort and Missing Set Cards read correctly at 1x, but the Deck Properties and About bodies are empty.

(4) Cupertino viewed light and dark at 1x and 1.5x. Sampled the other ready rows (popup_menu, page_control, combo_box, command_palette, card, toast, status_bar and others) for ink pixels under 3:1: only disabled items, hairlines and decorative arrows.

Honest limits. Not every Cupertino ready row outside the round was viewed; contrasts are nominal ink pairs at the pixel; the dialog bodies other than Search for Card, Options, Sort and Missing Set Cards were not judged.

| Flavor | Light | Dark | Delta against Audit 33 (light / dark) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Windows | 85 (85) | 77 (77) | 0 / 0 |
| Linux | 100 (100) | 100 (100) | 0 / 0 |
| Neper | 99 (100) | 99 (100) | -1 / -1 |

Honest limits. The comparer uses exact-colour components and excludes text; low-contrast fills (page-coloured bars) cannot be measured, so the Android top app bar height rests on the recorded 64 against 56 and the ink rows only. Dark rows were measured by role colours and component sizes, not cell by cell. The Flutter hover and pressed refs are mid-fade frames. A pill-edge profile fit meant to separate quadratic from arc corners was inconclusive on synthetic shapes and is not used; the circle and cap fits above are. Neper ready rows with recorded deviations (primary colour, 3 px bar rows) were not downgraded for those.

(5) Claimed matched without a measurement in the notes: the Cupertino memo, mask_edit, token_edit and button_edit notes are one copied sentence (only memo was visible in a sample; mask, token and button edit not separately measured); Android table, accordion, wizard, date_picker, banner and divider cite colours but I found no size measurement of the Neper and Android pairs beyond the recorded deviations (checked visually and by component size only); Neper date_edit calendar and image combo box (lane d admission); Cupertino check_list_box, time_picker and date_picker compare wheels and a 22 px circle that have no equivalent in the ref.

(6) Source fixes. Android icon button focus outline, Android progress track secondary-container, Cupertino wheel band, Cupertino action sheet cards (light) and Neper field hover darken render as claimed (numbers in (2)).

(7) Regression. `button_states`, `checkbox`, `list_box`, `linux_button`, `linux_list_box`, `linux_popup_menu`, `native_dark4`, `linux_text_edit`, `linux_tree_list`, `native_dark6` and `surf_overlay` re-rendered about 4,900 PNGs of all ten looks (every file rewritten); `git status` stayed clean, so the Windows, Linux, Neper, Material and Cupertino pictures are byte-identical to the committed ones, including everything the four hooks could touch.

Honest limits. The comparer excludes label width (button labels differ from the refs), uses exact-colour components so low-contrast fills (page-coloured bars) are not measurable, the Flutter hover and pressed refs are mid-fade or mid-ripple frames, and Cupertino text is excluded. I measured, not re-rendered, the Flutter refs. Neper ready rows were not downgraded by rule.

# Looks audit 28, 2026-11-01 (round 61: Neper popup menu sheets whole, fill centred, chevron hover scroll, section headers)

Independent recompute at master 76715f0e9 in a scratch worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe` (the shared tree was left alone, nothing was killed). New scratch comparers, not committed: a clip scan (background = the sheet's last pixel, menu = pixels more than 2 levels from it below the caption row, gaps to the four image edges), a fill scanner (a hovered fill = a run of 150 or more equal pixels in a row, at least 24 rows tall; the text ink inside it by row profile: cap top, x-height top and baseline), an ink centroid, a Windows capture placement and counting script (our sheet cropped at 19 x 21 offsets around (16, 35); counted = more than 2 levels in any channel; flat = a reference pixel whose eight neighbours are equal), a WCAG contrast probe, and a scratch copy of `popup_menu.e` with a 330 px limit to see the chevron strips (the committed sheets pass limit 0, so no sheet shows a strip). Rendered one app at a time: popup_menu (the default run and all 26 argument runs, which write Neper, Neper dark, Windows light and Windows dark, both scales) and 35 other apps. Everything re-renders byte for byte except one file pair (item 5).

Result. Two upgrades: popup_menu in Neper and Neper dark goes from review to ready (overlay `status.d/zzzz...-audit28.json`). Counts over the 30 Delphi equivalent controls (matched or ready / review / started): Windows light 20 / 9 / 1 (85.3 %), Windows dark 16 / 11 / 3 (77.3 %), Linux light and dark 30 / 0 / 0 (100 %), Neper light and dark 30 / 0 / 0 (100 %), macOS and iOS light 0 / 20 / 10 (46.7 %) and dark 0 / 28 / 2 (57.3 %), Android light 8 / 18 / 4 (65.3 %) and dark 8 / 19 / 3 (66.7 %). Overall 79.9 %: Windows 81.3, Linux 100, Neper 100, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 66.0.

(1) The test. `tests/selfhost/fixtures/link/ui_popup_menu_scroll` compiles for Windows and prints `ui popup menu scroll ok` (exit 0). Mutations in the scratch worktree (reverted, none committed): the hover gesture bit removed from the strips fails with exit 26; section headers off fails with 35; hover scroll speed 360 cut to 36 fails with 26; scroll direction swapped fails with 26. Removing the line that stops requesting frames when the view cannot move at an end does not fail it (exit 0): nothing tests the end state beyond the offset clamp. The test also does not touch the fill centring (row content height 32 px), the strips' visibility at the ends or the sheet cell sizing; those rest on pixel scans.

(2) Clipping. All 312 Neper and Neper dark popup PNGs (78 sheets, two palettes, 1x and 1.5x) have no menu pixel on any of the four image edges; the smallest gap to the right edge is 40 px (1x) and 60 px (1.5x), to the bottom 69 and 103 px. The Online, Admin and three child sheets that audit 27 found cut are whole. At 1.25x and 2x (dialog, file, view, online, sub_view_toolbars_child and sel_search_h5, both palettes, 24 files) the smallest gaps are 50 and 80 px right, 117 and 188 px bottom; the dialog at 2x and the file sheet at 1.25x draw all their rows (22 and 30, 26). The sheets hang the menu from the cell's top left by design, so left and top gaps are 16 and 7 px.

(3) Fill alignment, measured. 59 hovered fills per palette at 1x, each 32 px tall (31 where the anti aliased edge row is not an equal run). Against the fill centre the ink centroid is 1.70 px low (max 2.13) in Neper and 1.88 (max 2.64) in Neper dark, which confirms the 1.8 and corrects the 2.2 maximum for dark. By glyph geometry the cap height centre is 0.97 px low (sd 0.35) in light and 1.15 in dark, the x-height centre 2.32 and 2.50 low (cap top row 12, x-height top row 15, baseline row 21 of 32). So the claim that the remaining 1.8 px is lowercase mass is only partly true: about 0.8 to 1.3 px is the x-height bias and 1.0 px is a real offset of the capitals below the centre. Reference point: v3's own selected row (`ref/windows/popup_menu_sel_cards_h3.png`, 20 px fill) has its cap centre exactly on the fill centre, its x-height centre 1.5 px low and its centroid 0.9 px low. I accept 1.0 px for a Neper design (a line box centred in the row; the fill reads as centred on all the sheets I looked at); a baseline centred design would put the capitals on the centre and cost one pixel of text position. The 4 px error of audit 27 is gone. No test covers it.

(4) Sheets looked at (about 40 images, whole or in contact sheets, both palettes, 1x and 1.5x): popup_menu, hover, dialog, cards, file, view, online, admin, help, deck mode, reports, sel_cards_h3, sel_help_h2, sel_online_h1, sub_cards_print_child, sub_view_toolbars_child, sub_cards_export_parent, and the 2x and 1.25x files. Menus whole, rim and shadow inside the cell, shortcut column whole, separators, check marks, submenu arrows; the hovered fill is centred on its text and in dark leaves the one pixel outline in place (112 to 121 on the hovered row against 112 elsewhere). Section headers (Sort Order, Group By, Cards, Mode, General Filter) are dim small labels, 9 px of ink, contrast 7.5 to 1 in light and 9.2 to 1 in dark, with no key and so no region and no focus stop (the test pins that the first row after the header owns key 23 and nothing owns key 22). Chevron strips: in the scratch 330 px render of the Cards menu both strips are drawn at offset 0, so the up strip is visible where it can do nothing; an 8 row menu shows none. That is a small gap (the worker lists it), not a defect of the claim that strips appear where the menu overflows. Two gaps outside the claims: a flagged row that is disabled draws no fill in Neper (sel_cards_1 and _4, sel_file_1, sel_reports_1 and _4: five sheets per palette) where the v3 capture draws the blue row, and the parent submenu sheets still do not show the opened child.

(5) Other Neper controls. The row geometry change sits in `bar_menu_clamped` under the Neper profile, so it reaches every Neper menu that goes through `bar_menu`: the menu bar drop downs and submenus, not the tabs, lists or toolbar. 15 apps re-rendered (button, button_states, radio_states, radio_group_states, edits_states, check_list_box_states, group_box_dialog, status_bar, toolbar_button, list_box, combo_box, tree_list, page_control, native_dark5, menu_bar): their Neper and Neper dark files are byte identical to the committed ones except `neper-dark/menu_bar-open.png` and `@1.5x`, which differ from the commit (4213 and 7887 px, up to 197 levels; the last commit of the file is 2026-10-18): the drop down's text now sits centred in its 32 px rows, 6 px lower than the committed picture. The render is the better one, so no status moves, but the round 61 note that the sampled apps re-render byte for byte missed this sheet and the two PNGs are stale (not regenerated here, an audit does not edit). The light menu_bar files and every other open state sheet are identical.

(6) Windows light popup. The 28 raw captures at offset (16, 35), counted: Admin 4, Online sel 17, Help 32 and 46, Tools 54 and 54, Reports 60, sel_reports 55 and 59, deck mode 51, Search 116 and 115, Card Art 129, sel_cardart_1 188 and _3 146, sel_cards_1 and _4 137 and 135, sel_file_1 173 and _24 181, View 144 and 143 (sel_view_1 and _5), flat chrome pixels 0 in 24 of 28 and at most 15 in the others; the same figures as audit 27. The 10 Windows light apps (button, button_states, radio_states, radio_group_states, edits_states, check_list_box_states, group_box_dialog, status_bar, toolbar_button, list_box) compare byte for byte, so the 20 matched Windows light controls hold. The Windows light and dark popup sheets (the whole popup_menu app) and the Cupertino, Material and Linux popup sheets (cup_popup, cup_menu, material_menu, linux_popup_menu) are byte identical to the committed files.

(7) Regression sample, all byte identical: linux_button, _checkbox, _list_box, _popup_menu, _text_edit, _tree_list; cup_button, cup_menu, cup_popup, cup_tree_list; native_dark4, 6, 7 and icb (Windows dark); material_button, _checkbox, _list, _chips, _switch, _menu. Scale (`looks_scale_check.py`, exact / within a device pixel at 1.25, 1.5, 2.0): windows popup_menu_view 100 / 100 at all three, material:menu 99.9 / 100, 100 / 100, 100 / 100 (dark the same); both pass.

(8) Why ready. Audit 27 kept the Neper popup menu in review for four defects; all four are closed on my numbers (no clipped sheet, fill error 4.2 px down to 1.0 px from capital centred, a test that fails when the behaviour breaks, hover scroll and headers present). What remains is small: disabled selected rows without fill, both chevron strips always drawn, no test of the fill and the ends, and the stale menu_bar-open pair. None is a missing or broken claimed state, so ready stands with the gaps recorded on the rows.

Biggest gaps: macOS and iOS (46.7 and 57.3 %, no captures), Android review 37 of 60, Windows dark 11 review and 3 started, Windows light review 9 and started date_edit, regenerate `neper-dark/menu_bar-open.png`, the Neper disabled selected row and the always drawn up chevron.

# Looks audit 27, 2026-11-01 (round 59: Neper popup menu scroll and every row, hovered rows, Windows View picture, dark ampersand)

Independent recompute at master d0645895b in a scratch worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe` (the shared tree was left alone, nothing was killed). New scratch comparers, not committed: a row counter (ink bands in the label column of every Neper sheet against the item count parsed from `popup_menu.e`'s specs), a hover and selected row scanner, a clipping and corner scan, a placement and counting script for the Windows popup captures (our sheet searched at 18 x 15 offsets around (16, 35); counted = more than 2 levels in any channel; flat = a reference pixel whose eight neighbours are equal), and a widget harness program (`e.ui.looks` rig, `e.ui.testing` wheel, Tab and bounds) that builds a 26 row Neper menu through `bar_menu_at` and `bar_menu_limited`. Rendered one app at a time: popup_menu (all 78 sheet runs, which writes the Neper, Neper dark, Windows light and Windows dark popup files, both scales) and 31 other apps (the 10 Windows light apps of audit 26, 6 Linux, cup_button, cup_menu, cup_popup, cup_tree_list, 6 Material, native_dark4, 5, 6, 7 and icb); every re-render is byte identical to the committed PNGs (git status clean). Scale: popup_menu_file at 1.25 and popup_menu_dialog at 2 draw all 26, 22 and 30 rows in both Neper palettes.

Result. No upgrade, no downgrade (empty overlay `status.d/zzzz...-audit27.json`). Counts over the 30 Delphi equivalent controls (matched or ready / review / started): Windows light 20 / 9 / 1 (85.3 %), Windows dark 16 / 11 / 3 (77.3 %), Linux light and dark 30 / 0 / 0 (100 %), Neper light and dark 29 / 1 / 0 (98.7 %), macOS and iOS light 0 / 20 / 10 (46.7 %) and dark 0 / 28 / 2 (57.3 %), Android light 8 / 18 / 4 (65.3 %) and dark 8 / 19 / 3 (66.7 %). Overall 79.6 %: Windows 81.3, Linux 100, Neper 98.7, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 66.0.

(1) Neper and Neper dark popup_menu stay in review. What round 59 fixed holds. All 78 sheets in both palettes and both scales show every row: the 75 single menu sheets match the spec item count exactly (300 sheet checks), the dialog shows 22 and 30 (File 26, View 30, Search 23, Cards 22, Card Art 15 included), shortcut column, separators, checked rows, disabled rows and the submenu arrows are drawn, and a flagged row draws the hovered fill in every sheet except those whose flagged row is disabled (sel_cards_1 and _4, sel_file_1, sel_reports_1 and _4: a disabled row never takes the fill, which is a reasonable reading but is not what the round 59 note says). Scrolling works: in the harness a 26 row menu in a 400 px window is 364 px tall with two chevron strips, three wheel notches move the rows 120 px, the opposite wheel moves them back, the offset clamps at both ends, and Tab moves the focused row one row per press while the view follows it (the focused row stayed 302 to 307 px, inside the 347 px viewport, until the focus wrapped and the view returned to the top); `bar_menu_limited` with a 300 px room does the same and a 8 row menu shows no strips. No automated test covers any of it (nothing in tests or lib calls `bar_menu_limited` or drives a menu wheel), and I added none because I may not edit source. Not ready for these reasons. (a) The round 59 note says 0 clipped, but 8 of the 78 sheets in each palette clip the menu: popup_menu_online, popup_menu_sel_online_1, _h1 and _h2 cut the Ctrl+G shortcut at the right edge and show no right corners, popup_menu_admin shows the Debug row between two clip lines with no corners, and the three sub_*_child sheets (cards export, view show grid lines, view toolbars) are cut at the left, top and right, the Ctrl+E of the export child loses its right margin; the cause is the narrow Windows capture sized cell times 1.7, which does not fit a 256 to 320 px Neper menu. (b) The chevron strips are drawn at both ends whatever the offset and do not scroll on hover (a known gap). (c) The hovered fill is about 3 px low: from the text band centre to the fill centre is 3.5 to 5.5 px in 56 of the 59 measured sheets per palette (about 3 px for a plain row), so it covers a strip of the next row and leaves the top of its own bare. (d) The v3 section headers are still plain rows, and the popup_menu-hover sheet and the parent sheets show the highlight but not the opened child. (e) A selected, disabled row shows no fill.

(2) Cupertino, Material and Linux popup sheets. Byte identical to the committed files (cup_menu, cup_popup, material_menu, linux_popup_menu re-rendered); the round 59 commit touches only Neper, Neper dark, Windows and Windows dark PNGs. The Cupertino `selected` omission (the Cupertino branch of `bar_menu_clamped` never sets the hovered look for a flagged row) does not matter for any claimed state: no Cupertino sheet app passes a selected command, and the Cupertino popup rows are not above 14 rows. It is a latent gap if a Cupertino keyboard selection is ever shown.

(3) Windows light popup. The raw captures (the 17 menu-item-selected sheets, the deck mode sheet, Admin, Card Art, Help, Reports, Search, Tools: 24 re-measured at offset (16, 35) in every case) count: Admin 4, Online sel 17, Help 32 and 46, Tools 54 and 54, Reports 60, sel_reports 55 and 59, deck mode 51, Search 116 and 115, Card Art 129, sel_cardart_1 188 and _3 146, sel_cards_1 and _4 137 and 135, sel_file_1 173 and _24 181, View 144 (sel_view_1) and 143 (sel_view_5), flat chrome pixels 0 in 20 of 24 and at most 15 in the others. View now has the Messages picture (143 against 348 in audit 26, 317 before round 59); the last row no longer carries the grey image. The Windows light sheets of the 10 apps compare byte for byte, which covers the 20 matched controls. The submenu parent and child capture sheets are different captures (full open menus, other geometry) and are not comparable at this offset; I did not count them. The status stands.

(4) Windows dark popup. The ampersand is literal now (`Sort by Edition & Card Name` draws the ampersand, `Show Grid Lines` and the other accelerators underline the letter); the shortcut column is whole; Sort and Messages rows still use the generic picture and the headers are not bold (unchanged, popup_menu rests on native_dark5 which re-renders identically). No status moves.

(5) Regression sample, all byte identical: the 10 Windows light apps (their Neper dark non popup outputs included), linux_button, _checkbox, _list_box, _popup_menu, _text_edit, _tree_list, cup_button, cup_menu, cup_tree_list, cup_popup, native_dark4, 5, 6, 7 and icb, material_button, _checkbox, _list, _chips, _switch, _menu.

Independent recompute at master e8275eb5d in a scratch worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe` (the shared tree was left alone, nothing was killed). New scratch comparers, not committed: a placement and counting script for the Windows popup captures (our sheet cropped at the offset (16, 35) and searched over 11 x 9 offsets; counted = more than 2 levels in any channel; flat = a reference pixel whose eight neighbours are equal), a blank scan and a hover difference scan for the Neper sheets. Rendered one app at a time: popup_menu (all 39 runs), 10 Windows light apps (button, button_states, radio_states, radio_group_states, edits_states, check_list_box_states, group_box_dialog, status_bar, toolbar_button, list_box), 6 Linux, 3 Cupertino, 6 Material, native_dark4, 6, 7 and icb; every re-render is byte identical to the committed PNGs (git status clean), which also covers the 126 Neper and Neper dark files of the 10 Windows light apps and the Windows dark ones. Scale: popup_menu_cards and popup_menu_view at 1.5 pass 100 / 100.

Result. Two downgrades: popup_menu in Neper and Neper dark goes back to review (overlay `status.d/zzzz...-audit26.json`). Counts over the 30 Delphi equivalent controls (matched or ready / review / started): Windows light 20 / 9 / 1 (85.3 %), Windows dark 16 / 11 / 3 (77.3 %), Linux light and dark 30 / 0 / 0 (100 %), Neper light and dark 29 / 1 / 0 (98.7 %), macOS and iOS light 0 / 20 / 10 (46.7 %) and dark 0 / 28 / 2 (57.3 %), Android light 8 / 18 / 4 (65.3 %) and dark 8 / 19 / 3 (66.7 %). Overall 79.6 %: Windows 81.3, Linux 100, Neper 98.7, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 66.0.

(1) Neper popup_menu. The blank sheets are fixed: all 78 Neper and 78 Neper dark sheets, both scales, draw a menu (fewest pixels off the page colour below the caption 10608 and 9792, the one row Admin menu). The shortcut column, separators, disabled rows, check marks and the Print and Export arrows are drawn and the dark column is whole. But the menu is not complete. `navigation.bar_menu_at` returns `TooLarge` when `commands.len > 14` for every profile except Windows (Windows clips over a bottom scroll arrow, `bar_menu_limited`), and the sheet app passes room = 14, so the longer menus are cut to their first 14 entries with no scroll arrow and no hint: File (26 entries) loses everything after Mode (Deck from Card Pool, Toggle Mode, Import, Update Prices, Full Screen, Minimize, Stay on Top, Exit), View (30) stops after Card Rules, Search (23), Cards (22, no Paste, Select All, Print, Export, Lock Collection) and Card Art (15) are cut too: 45 of the 78 sheets show a truncated menu, among them the Print and Export parent sheets, whose submenu arrow is one of the missing rows, and the View check rows. This is a cap in the control, not a design decision (it errors, it does not scroll), so ready does not hold; both go to review. Also in the sheets: the hover sheet popup_menu-hover (its cell was not enlarged) clips the menu at the left, top and right and highlights no row; every `_h` and `_sel_` sheet equals its rest sheet below the caption row (only the title text differs, 330 to 435 px), so no Neper sheet shows a hovered or selected row (the pointer is parked off the sheet); v3's section headers (Mode, Sort Order, Group By, Cards, General Filter) are drawn as plain items.

(2) Windows light popup, own placement. The 28 raw captures still reproduce at the offset (16, 35), counted: Admin 4, Online 17, Help 32, Tools 54, Reports 60, Search 116, CardArt 129, Cards 145 (153 before), File 175, sel_cardart_1 188, sel_file_1 and _24 173 and 181, sel_search 115 to 116, Export child 6, 0 to 15 flat in every sheet. One regression of round 57: View is 348 (163 in audit 25), 209 of them in the last row. Adding the dfm accelerator to Messages (`&Messages`) breaks the picture lookup in `winmenuicon.e`, which matches the label exactly (`"Messages"`), so the row draws the generic grey image instead of v3's clipboard with the red cross; every View sheet (view, sel_view_*, the two View parents) carries it. Not enough to move the status (the whole control is still inside the glyph and picture tolerance used for the Sort pictures), it is an open gap with a one line fix (key the lookup on the label without marks, or add the `&Messages` spelling).

(3) Windows dark. The two audit 25 defects are fixed: Cards and View sheets are wide enough, the shortcut column is whole (Ctrl+Alt+C, Ctrl+Alt+Shift+Del), and File shows Print Proxies disabled. Remaining, not moving the status (popup_menu windows_dark rests on native_dark5): in View the single `&` of `Sort by Edition & Card Name` is read as an accelerator mark (the dark sheet draws `Edition _Card Name`, the light sheet and the capture draw the ampersand), the headers are not bold, and the Sort and Messages rows have the generic picture.

(4) Regression sample, all byte identical: the 10 Windows light apps, Linux linux_button, linux_checkbox, linux_list_box, linux_popup_menu, linux_text_edit, linux_tree_list, Cupertino cup_button, cup_menu, cup_tree_list, Neper dark non popup sheets (the same 10 apps' Neper dark outputs), Windows dark native_dark4, 6, 7 and icb, Material material_button, _checkbox, _list, _chips, _switch, _menu. No other claim moves.

Biggest gaps: macOS and iOS (46.7 and 57.3 %, no captures), Android review 37 of 60, Windows dark 11 review and 3 started, Windows light review 9 and started date_edit, the Neper popup menu 14 row cap and invisible hover, the Windows View Messages picture, the Sort list pictures.

# Looks audit 25, 2026-11-01 (round 55: image combo box lists, popup menu rest sheets)

Independent recompute at master b7859dfa7 in a scratch worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe` (the shared tree was left alone, nothing was killed). New scratch comparers, not committed, none of the scripts the brief named: a placement and counting library (our sheet located by least squared difference of the raw 1:1 capture, counted = more than 2 levels in any channel, glyph zone = components of strong edges 5 to 16 px tall grown 2 px, flat = a reference pixel in a run of 11 equal pixels with all eight neighbours also in runs), a cell comparer for same size composites and a blank sheet scan. Rendered one app at a time: popup_menu (all 61 runs), image_combo_box (25), 14 Windows light apps, native_dark4/6/7/icb, 6 Linux, 3 Cupertino, 6 Material.

Result. The two Windows light claims hold (no overlay entry, they stay matched), and one new finding moves Neper: popup_menu ready to review in Neper and Neper dark (overlay `status.d/zzzz...-audit25.json`). Counts over the 30 Delphi equivalent controls (matched or ready / review / started): Windows light 20 / 9 / 1 (85.3 %), Windows dark 16 / 11 / 3 (77.3 %), Linux light and dark 30 / 0 / 0 (100 %), Neper light and dark 29 / 1 / 0 (98.7 %), macOS and iOS light 0 / 20 / 10 (46.7 %) and dark 0 / 28 / 2 (57.3 %), Android light 8 / 18 / 4 (65.3 %) and dark 8 / 19 / 3 (66.7 %). Overall 79.6 %: Windows 81.3, Linux 100, Neper 98.7, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 66.0.

(a) The edited item specs against the v3 sources (`version-3/src/mtgstudio/ui/vcl/main.vcl.form.dfm`, read only). The library draws the states: the 28 captures mix enabled, disabled, checked, picture and submenu rows and all reproduce. The captions of Cards, Online and View are v3's (every one is a Caption in the dfm except Copy as Text, Show Summary Footer and Messages, which differ at run time; the `&&` of the Sort by items is the dfm's). The enabled flags are not a v3 constant: each action's OnUpdate depends on the view, the editing mode, a selected card and the lock (acCardsAddToCollectionUpdate, acCardsAddFoilToCollectionUpdate, acCardsSelectAllUpdate, acOnlineGathererUpdate), so Cards and Online are the menus of the captured state (card selected, collection editing), not a canonical one. The shortcut column also follows the maintainer's setup, not the dfm (Messages is ShortCut 24653, Ctrl+Shift+M, and the capture shows Ctrl+M; Minimize is 16461, Ctrl+M, the capture Ctrl+Alt+M; v3 reassigns them from its Shortcuts dialog). So the sheets show the real menus in the captured state. Four accelerator marks are wrong, 8 to 9 px each, and the worker's edit dropped one: `Messages` lacks the dfm's `&M` (capture underlines the M), `Show Summary Footer` lacks the underlined F, `Copy as Text` lacks the underlined T, and `Conditional Formattin&g...` draws an underline the capture does not have.

(b) Re-measure, own placement. The 28 raw popup captures (10 dialogs menu-*, 15 menu-item-selected-*, menu-reports-deck-mode, the two Export submenu grabs): counted 4 to 188, every one at the same offset (16, 35), flat chrome 0 except 6 px of the Exit row in the two File sheets (row 573, x 33 to 38). Cards 153 (44 outside the glyph zone), Online 17, View 163 (8), File 175, Search 116, CardArt 129, Reports 60, Tools 54, Admin 4, Help 32, sel_cardart_1 188, deck mode 51, Export parent 153 and child 6; the three edited ones changed from 2802 / 2998 / 2759 as claimed and the View menu is 626 px with its Messages row in every Windows sheet. The ten image combo lists against their raw files: list_art 19 (12 outside), list_art2 19, list_art3 19, list_auth 13, list_auth2 13, list_condition 22, 22, 22, 22 (condition, 2, 3, 4), all 0 flat; list_sort 1495 / 142 / 0 flat at the true placement (a squared difference placer picks one row lower, 1908, 137 flat; the row is text). What the 1495 is: 1353 inside the glyph zone, of which 380 are the two row pictures (190 each: ours is a grey gear, v3 draws the a-z and z-a sort bitmaps), 280 the None row text over the blue bar and 819 the Ascending and Descending text; the text ink boxes agree within 1 px. No artwork tiles. The other nine lists have no differing picture, so the Sort pictures are the one open picture gap of the combo, the same class as the 140 px cell picture.

(c) Windows dark. The three rest sheets differ from the committed ones before round 55 in exactly the edited rows (Cards at Add to Collection, Add Foil to Collection, Select All and Lock Collection, all four Online rows, View at the checks, the Group Box and Messages rows); size unchanged. They are sane as derived pictures and the dark status does not rest on them (popup_menu windows_dark points at native_dark5, the native flyout captures, byte identical). Two defects that were there before and are not round 55's, no status moved: the dark Cards and View menus are wider than the 261 and 258 px sheets, so the shortcut column is clipped (Ctr, Ctrl+Alt), and the dark File sheet draws Print Proxies enabled where the light sheet and the capture have it disabled.

(d) The 14 Windows light apps (button, button_states, radio_states, radio_group_states, edits_states, check_list_box_states, group_box_dialog, status_bar, toolbar_button, list_box, token_edit_dialog, hint, label, scroll_bar) re-render byte for byte as the committed PNGs in Windows, Windows dark, Neper and Neper dark, as do popup_menu and image_combo_box (git status clean after every run). Sources since audit 24 changed only examples/looks/popup_menu.e. Re-measures on composite cells: button_states 323 / 53 / 0 (33 cells), edits_states 124 / 0 / 0 (16), radio_states 43 / 0 / 0 (8; ours draws the radio group panel wider than the captured crop), check_list_box_states 269 / 38 / 2 (7), token_edit_dialog 0. Regression, all identical to committed: native_dark4, 6, 7 and native_dark_icb; Linux linux_button, linux_checkbox, linux_list_box, linux_popup_menu, linux_text_edit, linux_tree_list; Cupertino cup_button, cup_menu, cup_tree_list; Material material_button, _checkbox, _list, _chips, _switch, _menu. Scale (looks_scale_check): popup_menu_cards 1.5 100 / 100, image_combo_box_list_sort 1.5 100 / 100, popup_menu_view 2.0 100 / 100, pass.

(e) New finding, Neper. The status picture of popup_menu in Neper and Neper dark is blank: 77 of 78 Neper popup sheets and 76 of 78 Neper dark sheets have nothing below the caption (only popup_menu-hover, and in dark popup_menu, draw menus, with the shortcut column clipped in dark); blank since 2026-10-15 (e6a15847b, 39951d6d3), and menu_bar-open (Neper) draws no list under its highlighted title. Ready cannot stand on a blank picture: both go to review. Neper 29 of 30 ready now.

# Looks audit 24, 2026-11-01 (round 53: image combo box open lists, menu bar hot reference, File menu)

Independent recompute at master 6660863d3 in a scratch worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe` (the shared tree was left alone, nothing was killed). New scratch comparers, not committed, none of the scripts the brief named: a placement and counting library (template match of our crop in all 705 raw 1:1 captures under `ref/windows-1to1`, counted = more than 2 levels in any channel, glyph zone = components of strong edges 5 to 16 px tall grown 2 px, flat = a reference pixel in a run of 11 equal pixels with all eight neighbours also in runs), a menu bar strip placer and a popup window placer. Rendered: 22 Windows apps (259 windows, 176 windows-dark, 176 neper and 176 neper-dark PNG rewritten), 6 Linux (19 + 19 PNG), 3 Cupertino (6 + 6), 6 Material (18 + 18) and the dialog app.

Result. Two downgrades, no upgrade: image_combo_box and popup_menu, matched to review (overlay `status.d/zzzz...-audit24.json`); menu_bar holds. Counts over the 30 Delphi equivalent controls: Windows light matched 18, review 11, started 1 (82.7 %); Windows dark matched 16, review 11, started 3 (77.3 %); Linux light and dark matched 30 (100 %); Neper light and dark ready 30 (100 %); macOS and iOS light review 20, started 10 (46.7 %), dark review 28, started 2 (57.3 %); Android light matched 8, review 18, started 4 (65.3 %), dark matched 8, review 19, started 3 (66.7 %). Overall 79.6 %: Windows 80.0, Linux 100, Neper 100, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 66.0.

(1) image_combo_box, review. Counted / outside glyph zone / flat chrome against the raw captures: the six open lists are clean, list_art 19 / 12 / 0, list_art2 19, list_auth and list_auth2 13 / 0 / 0, list_condition and list_condition2 22 / 0 / 0 (the claim holds; the frame is whole pixel). Heads 2 to 6, hover drop and hover edit 6, cell and held cell 140 (the 16 x 16 picture), as claimed. The claim that list_art3, list_condition3, list_condition4 and list_sort have no raw capture is wrong: `hover-combo-list-options-card-art-position-row2`, `hover-combo-list-options-condition-row2` and `-row3` and `image-combo-box-open-list-sort` are in `states`. Against them: list_art3 1251 / 633 / 176 (the 176 are a 3 level ghost, 252,253,255 over 255, inside the frame: round 53 removed it from the rest lists only), list_condition3 4033 / 551 / 0, list_condition4 3699 / 217 / 0, list_sort 1495 / 142 / 0 (outside-glyph px are the v3 pictures). One captured state with flat chrome over tolerance: review until the hover-row lists use the same whole pixel path. combo_box (review, unchanged): the colour lists did improve, list_color 2086 to 1254 px and flat 375 to 0, list_color2 1697 to 1253 and 383 to 0 against `combo-box-open-list-options-row-color`; the show and size rest lists are 0 flat; the hover-row lists still carry 2 to 19 flat ghost px (colour3, show3 to 5, size3, size5), the same defect as art3.

(2) menu_bar holds. The reference change is sound: in `main-window@1x` (rest) no title is raised, in `states/hover-menubar-title-view` View is raised, so the old tie went to a rest grab with a different state. Placing our ten hot strips (rows 32 to 58 of menu_bar_hot) in the raw files: every strip equals its own `hover-menubar-title-<name>` at 9 px, 0 flat (the View strip too), and the rest grab is 84 to 138 flat px off for every title (114 for View), so the hot cell does correspond to the hover capture. The old and new composites differ in exactly one 114 px cell per sheet (View, 771,198 and 622,30); no other cell was reinterpreted. Also: the rest strip 9 px, hover and pressed Cards 1 px, the nine open sheets' bars 9 px (0 flat); menu_bar_open_admin has no open capture (unverified, minor). popup_menu, review: of 28 distinct raw popup captures 25 reproduce at 4 to 188 px with 0 flat (File 175 with Print Proxies disabled, as claimed; Reports 60, Tools 54, Search 116, CardArt 129, Help 32, Admin 4, all menu-item-selected and the Export submenus 6 to 181). Three rest menus do not: popup_menu_cards 2802 / 58 / 0 (the 1:1 items are enabled, ours disabled), popup_menu_online 2998 / 235 / 0 (same), popup_menu_view 2259 / 664 / 350 (ours is the old 618 px menu clipped with a scroll arrow, the 1:1 menu is 626 px with a Messages row; the sel_view sheets already draw 626 px). They equal the old crisp captures (33, 174, 167 px), the same old against 1:1 conflict round 53 settled for File in favour of the 1:1 capture; the Cards and Online items differ only by state, View by geometry.

(3) The 17 earlier matched Windows light controls hold. Every PNG rendered (apps button, button_states, button_hover, button_shift, radio_states, radio_hover, radio_group_states, radio_group_dialog, edits_states, edits_hover, edits_dialog, check_list_box, check_list_box_states, group_box_dialog, status_bar, toolbar_button, list_box, token_edit_dialog, hint, plus dialog) is byte for byte the committed file, in Windows, Windows dark, Neper and Neper dark, with one family exception: combo_box_flat and combo_box_flat_states (windows, windows-dark, neper; neper-dark has no committed file) differ in text pixels only (committed is greyscale, the run is ClearType fringed; rows 41 to 50), a run argument or text mode artifact, not round 53 (it touches no Neper sheet). The stale dialog and dialog_wide sheets are fixed (byte equal). Spot re-measures on composite cells: button_states 323 / 53 / 0 (33 cells), edits_states 124 / 66 / 0 (16), radio_states 38 / 0 / 0, check_list_box_states 269 / 42 / 0, all as audit 23.

(4) Regression: Linux 6 apps (19 + 19 PNG), Cupertino 3 (6 + 6), Material 6 (18 + 18), Windows dark and Neper dark 176 each: all identical to committed. Scale 1.5 (looks_scale_check): button 83.0 exact / 100.0 within a device pixel, image_combo_box 98.7 / 100.0, pass.

text_edit and memo: no TcxTextEdit, TcxMemo or TMemo exists in the v3 captures, so they can never be measured against v3. Policy: they stay review (capped, honest) with the native Windows 11 light gallery as a plausibility reference only, not a v3 match; a native-themed edit is not v3's DevExpress edit (frame 8 to 15 levels off). Only a maintainer acceptance, as for the splitter, could lift them to matched; flagged, not assumed.

Biggest gaps: macOS and iOS (46.7 and 57.3 %, no captures), Android review 37 of 60, Windows light review 11 (text_edit, memo, combo_box, image_combo_box, popup_menu, progress_bar, page_control, tree_list, vertical_grid, data_grid, dialog) and started date_edit, Windows dark 11 review and 3 started.

# Looks audit 23, 2026-11-01 (round 51: crisp button, radio and group geometry became the Windows light default)

Independent recompute at master aba5fbd20 (the shared tree is on another branch with another session's uncommitted edits; I left them and worked in a scratch worktree of master with the committed `neper-self.exe`, killing nothing). New scratch comparers, not committed, none of the scripts the brief named: `a23m.py` (cells of a same size 1:1 composite are its connected non background parts, shrunk by the 2 px margin and counted once where cells nest; counted = more than 2 levels in any channel; glyph zone = components of strong edges 5 to 16 px tall grown 2 px; flat = a reference pixel in a run of 11 equal pixels with all eight neighbours also in runs), `a23place.py` and `a23task2.py` (placement of a changed control in the raw 1:1 captures under `ref/windows-1to1/dialogs`, `states` and `main-window`: the pixels that differ between old and new are masked out, a textured patch of the unchanged context is template matched in all 705 raw files, candidates are kept when the unchanged pixels of the control box agree, and old and new are then counted against each raw capture at that place; when the caption moved too, patches of the old and the new render are pooled so neither is favoured), `a23cell.py` and `a23icb.py` (a whole cell of a sheet placed by pixel equality, with ring and row figures). All 176 sheet apps were compiled and run one or two at a time, then every argument run of `scripts/looks_scale_sheets.json` (187) plus the scroll bar, status bar and toolbar arguments it does not list.

Result. No upgrade and no downgrade. Counts over the 30 Delphi equivalent controls: Windows light matched 17, review 12, started 1 (81.3 %); Windows dark matched 16, review 11, started 3 (77.3 %); Linux light and dark matched 30 (100 %); Neper light and dark ready 30 (100 %); macOS and iOS light review 20, started 10 (46.7 %), dark review 28, started 2 (57.3 %); Android light matched 8, review 18, started 4 (65.3 %), dark matched 8, review 19, started 3 (66.7 %). Overall 79.5 %: Windows 79.3, Linux 100, Neper 100, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 66.0. The overlay `status.d/zzzz...-audit23.json` is empty on purpose.

(1) The 17 matched Windows light controls hold. Every PNG the apps write, in all ten looks (Windows 624 files of 937 rewritten by name, Windows dark 448, Neper 448, Neper dark 448, Linux 103, Linux dark 103, Cupertino 68, Cupertino dark 68, Material 82, Material dark 82; the rest are -diff, scale and orphan files no app writes) is byte for byte the committed one, with one exception: `dialog` and `dialog_wide` (each .png, @1.5x and -vs-ref, 6 files). Those two sheets hold buttons, the default switch changed them (5922 and 3254 px against the committed file, which still shows the blurred ring; rendering master~1 reproduces the committed file, so the change is round 51's), and the round 51 commit did not regenerate them. The committed `dialog.png` and `dialog_wide.png` are stale and a later round should re-render them (both are review controls, no status moves). The matched figures against the true 1:1 composites reproduce, counted / outside glyph zone / flat: button 36 / 20 / 0, button_states 323 / 311 / 0 (33 cells), button_hover 453 / 395 / 1, button_kinds 549 / 312 / 15 (the arrow button's picture), button_shift 2 / 1 / 0, button_arrows 485 / 485 / 16 (the pictures), radio_states 43, radio_hover 136, radio_group_dialog 120 (91), radio_group_states 293, radio_dialog 894 of which 864 are the old resampled Options crop the worker excludes, token_edit_dialog 0, edits_dialog 56, edits_states 124 (112), edits_hover 89, check_list_box_dialog 284, _hover 197, _states 269, list_box 355, _blur 213, _focus 213 (the cut bottom edge, as before), toolbar_button, _row and _hot 0 over 166 cells, image combo box heads 2 to 6. group_box_dialog is 1375 here (the worker and audit 22 say 1552; my count takes each pixel once where a frame cell holds its radios, and my glyph zone is wider: 646 outside it, 0 flat). The status bar bars are 51 px off in the 784 wide bar (captions), report_11 is 0. The hint_toolbar sheets are 3 to 10 px off the hover-hint captures; hint_grid_header_name has no placeable capture (unchanged since audit 22). Scale check: button at 1.5 is 83.0 exact and 100.0 within a device pixel at master and at master~1 alike (the switch changed nothing), button at 2.0 100.0, radio at 1.5 100.0, group_box at 1.5 98.8, all pass. windows_crisp is not scale gated: the marks go through winpx and are exact at 1.5 and 2.0 as measured.

(2) The 15 changed unclaimed sheets, and the two stale ones, old against new, located in the raw captures. Where a control could be placed (context agreement 0.6 or more) the pixels the switch changed moved toward the capture: over the v3 based sheets 15,269 changed pixels now agree with the raw capture that did not before, 1,854 stopped agreeing, 9,870 agree with neither, and the pixels off the best captured state fell from 32,642 to 19,227. Per sheet, off in the control boxes old to new (gained / lost): scale_button 3778 to 1452 (2327 / 1; rest buttons 546 to 1 and 590 to 0), dialog 5351 to 990 (4545 / 184), dialog_wide 1888 to 820 (1250 / 182), dialog_more 6615 to 3349 (3337 / 71; a rest button 617 to 1), choices_filter 1325 to 688 (693 / 56; 12 of its 14 boxes are 3 px rules or have no usable context), dialog_message 670 to 335 (339 / 4), radio 3128 to 2274 (1138 / 284; the discs of rest and hover, 112 and 128 px each, went to 0, the focus rectangle rows are the rest), radio_group 3683 to 3263 (538 / 118), dialog_more2 1123 to 1024 (296 / 197), group_box 5016 to 4395 (658 / 37; a header bar the capture clips, so edge pixels only). Not moved or not settled: hint 4616 to 4542 (673 / 599, anchor buttons whose state has no capture), dialog_more3 465 to 490 (133 / 158, the Close button is drawn in the accent state and the nearest capture is a rest button), date_edit (the four Today and Clear buttons of the calendar popups changed, 1272 px; v3 has no capture of a date edit, so nothing can be measured and the control stays started), dialog_native, native_dark, native_dark2 and native_dark3 (native Windows 11 gallery sheets, not v3; no v3 capture applies and any placement there is a coincidence, not counted). So the default switch moved the unclaimed sheets closer to the 1:1 captures wherever a capture of the state exists, by about eight to one in changed pixels; no capture shows a sheet moved away. The worker's inconclusive count mixed states that have no capture with those that have.

(3) image_combo_box stays review, and the claim needs two corrections. The hovered row is right: the list_condition2 hovered row (rows 78, 79, 92 and 93 of audit 22) is pixel equal to the raw `combo-box-open-list-options-condition-row2` capture. The raw popup captures are crisp (their frame is a whole 0,120,215 row and column; audit 21 and the README were wrong that the popups are resampled, only their flat interior equals the old file). But the six open lists are not clean against them: ours draws the right column and the bottom row of the frame as half tone (128,188,235 and 85,165,228 where the capture has 0,120,215; the old resampled reference has the same soft edge), so list_condition and _condition2 are 573 px off (542 flat chrome), list_art 236 (180 on the frame), list_art2 234 (150 of them a 3 level ghost row under the top border, 252,253,255 against 255), and only list_auth and _auth2 are clean (13 px, 0 flat). list_art3, list_condition3, list_condition4 and list_sort have no raw capture (unverified), and the open_* sheets (head plus list) do not place as one piece. The heads, closed cell, held cell, dialog, hover_drop and hover_edit are 2 to 13 px off (cell and held 140, the picture), as claimed. Matched needs every captured state; the soft frame edge on four of the six captured lists says review. Fix: draw the list frame in whole pixels, as the heads are.

(4) Regression. The whole set above is the sample: Linux 103, Linux dark 103, Cupertino 68, Cupertino dark 68, Neper dark 448, Windows dark 448, Material 82 and Material dark 82 files, all identical to the committed ones. Three run artifacts that are not regressions: argument runs write the same sheet name from several arguments, so `linux_popup_menu` with `0` after `active` and `arrow` leaves popup_menu-hover different until the arguments run in sorted order (render_looks_all.sh does; checked); `scroll_bar` with `m`, `mh` and `x y` writes neper and windows dark files and a windows scroll_bar_states that differ identically at master~1; a few argument runs aborted (exit 134) while two other apps ran and passed alone. Scale check, one sheet at a time: button at 1.5 and 2.0, radio and group_box at 1.5, as in (1).

Biggest gaps: macOS and iOS (46.7 and 57.3 %, 32 of 60 controls only started or review with no capture), Android review 37 of 60, Windows light review 12 (text_edit, memo, combo_box, image_combo_box, progress_bar, page_control, menu_bar, popup_menu, tree_list, vertical_grid, data_grid, dialog) and Windows dark started 3; plus the stale dialog and dialog_wide sheets.

# Looks audit 22, 2026-11-01 (rounds 46 to 49 merged: Windows light bars, buttons, radios, edits, list box; token edit and image combo box fixes)

Independent recompute at master 20bccfcd1 (the shared tree sits on another branch with another session's uncommitted lib/e/fmt and lib/e/js edits; I left them, worked in a scratch worktree of master with the committed `neper-self.exe`, and killed nothing). New scratch comparers, not committed, none of the scripts the brief named: `a22.py` (cells of a reference composite are its connected non background parts, each measured at the same offset in ours; counted = ours differs by more than 2 levels in any channel; glyph zone = components of strong edges 5 to 16 px tall grown 2 px; flat = a reference pixel in a run of 11 equal pixels with all eight neighbours also in runs), `place.py` (FFT placement for the sheets whose layout differs: the bar sheets), `rawcheck.py` (looks each composite cell up, pixel for pixel, in the raw captures under `ref/windows-1to1/dialogs`, `states` and `main-window`), `rows.py` and `diffmap.py`. No `status.d` file named round48 or round49 exists on master (rounds 48 and 49 changed no status); the claims audited are those of `*round46-a/b/c*` and the two commit messages.

Result. No upgrade and no downgrade: every claim holds at the status it makes. Counts over the 30 Delphi-equivalent controls: Windows light matched 17, review 12, started 1 (81.3 %); Windows dark matched 16, review 11, started 3 (77.3 %); Linux light and dark matched 30 (100 %); Neper light and dark ready 30 (100 %); macOS and iOS light review 20, started 10 (46.7 %), dark review 28, started 2 (57.3 %); Android light matched 8, review 18, started 4 (65.3 %), dark matched 8, review 19, started 3 (66.7 %). Overall 79.5 %: Windows 79.3, Linux 100, Neper 100, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 66.0. The overlay `status.d/zzzz...-audit22.json` is empty on purpose (upgrades and downgrades only).

(1) The eleven Windows light claims, against the true 1:1 composites. First the references: every cell of the 26 composites I used (button, _states, _hover, _kinds, _shift, _arrows, group_box_dialog, radio_states, _hover, radio_group_dialog, _states, edits_dialog, _states, _hover, list_box_dialog, _blur, _focus, token_edit_dialog, status_bar, _hover, _report, _11, _167, toolbar_button, _row, _hot) is found pixel for pixel in a raw 1:1 capture, so the composites were not edited toward our render, with one exception: the 280 x 120 Options crop of `radio_dialog` is the old resampled capture (the worker says so; I do not count it, 864 px off). Counted (all more than 2 levels, glyphs included) / outside the glyph zone / flat chrome px off, per sheet: button 36 / 0 / 0, button_states 323 / 72 / 0 (33 of 33 cells), button_hover 453 / 72 / 0, button_kinds 549 / 274 / 5 (the five are the arrow button's picture, v3 artwork, excluded as in every earlier audit), button_shift 2 / 0 / 0, button_arrows 485 / 34 / 0 (the pictures); group_box_dialog 1552 / 352 / 0 (captions, the page icon of the header, not drawn, and ClearType); radio_states 43 / 23 / 0, radio_hover 136 / 46 / 0, radio_dialog first crop 18; radio_group_dialog 120 / 35 / 0, radio_group_states 293 / 130 / 0; token_edit_dialog 0 / 0 / 0 (all three crops); edits_dialog 56 / 52 / 0, edits_states 124 / 99 / 0, edits_hover 89 / 82 / 0 (spin, mask and button edit: every captured rest, focus, pressed, hover, selected and caret crop); list_box 355, 213 and 213 with the two bottom rows of the 154 row crops, 54, 62 and 62 without them (the crop is cut there and ours draws the closing edge, so the bottom edge is unverified, as the worker says); toolbar_button 0, _row 0, _hot 0 over 11 cells; status_bar 835 (772 of them the window frame row below the 18 row bar that the crop carries, 60 glyph zone, 3 fringe pixels), _hover 4 identical bars, _report 0, _11 0, _167 121 (digits). All numbers equal the workers'. The menu_bar review figures also reproduce (menu_bar_hot 88 flat chrome px in one cell, menu_bar_hot_view 106): review stays; popup_menu stays review as the worker says (not re-measured, no change claimed).

Rule for uncaptured states: matched needs every state that has a 1:1 capture within tolerance and the control's rest state captured; states with no capture are listed as unverified, which the ladder allows, and a control whose rest has no capture stays at most review. Applied: all eleven hold. Unverified lists I accept: button toggle, split, loading and plain kinds and the hover of the picture buttons (its capture is pixel equal to rest, 0 px, but no sheet renders ours); group box disabled, plain, empty, long caption; radio disabled; token edit with tokens, focused, filled (only the empty edit was ever captured: the weakest matched row, kept because the ladder asks for captured states only); spin edit disabled, read-only, invalid (guesses); list box bottom edge, disabled, hover row; toolbar and status bar states not captured. Stamps: drawn, not stamped: group frames, edit and spin frames, rules, rims, focus rectangles (XOR dots), grip strokes and separators are rectangles. Baked, all small: radio marks 4 tiles of 13 x 13, button rings 5 kinds (rest, accent, hover, pressed, disabled) of four 6 x 6 corner tiles (winring, winbutton), spin button corner blocks 2 x 2, arrow alpha 28 values, ellipsis weights 192, chevron 60; the toolbar picture atlas is 7344 px (28 icons, v3 artwork baked from the main window capture, exact by construction, not evidence for the control and not counted as such). No large baked region in winbutton.e, winedit.e, winmark.e (largest table 196 px), winradio.e or wintile.e. Constants fitted to single captures: disabled caption #838383, the focused token foot rows on the shortened box, the status bar grip stroke phase (x + y mod 4), the ellipsis weights. Finding that moves no status: the crisp ring, `set_radio_crisp` and `set_group_crisp` are opt-in per sheet app (the button, radio and group sheets switch them on, the library default keeps the old blurred ring), so the matched figures describe those sheet apps, not what `Look.Windows` draws for an application that does not opt in; a later round should flip the default.

(2) The surviving matched light controls and the shared edit frame. The 19 audit 20 controls minus audit 21's 13 downgrades are label, checkbox, check_list_box, splitter, hint and scroll_bar (6); their sheets (all apps) re-render byte for byte as the committed PNGs and none of their committed PNGs changed since audit 21 (git diff 113e0749a..master: no change), so they hold. text_edit and memo (review) changed with worker B's shared frame; v3 has no capture of a plain text edit or memo, so I measured the frame they share with the captured edits: the four 6 x 6 corners and the top and bottom three rows of text_edit rest, hover and focus against the rest, hover and focus edits of edits_dialog, edits_hover and edits_states: 0 px off at rest and in hover, 4 px in the focus corners (the capture's caret column); memo top three rows 0 px in all three states. Frame only: the sheets' text, disabled, read-only, invalid, placeholder and selection have no capture, so both stay review (no change; the nearest candidates for a next upgrade). date_edit (started) and combo_box re-render equal. Round 49's combo_hover redraw is right: its four changed cells are 6, 6, 2 and 5 px off the raw 1:1 hover captures (they were 1422, 1405, 1754 and 952 before) and fail only against the old resampled `ref/windows/combo_hover.png` (up to 668 flat px), which is stale. image_combo_box stays review, but not for the worker's reason: the open drop lists are not resampled (README, round 46), and I compared the 11 list sheets with the old, crisp popup captures, flat chrome px off 0 in all, yet the hovered row's top and bottom edge rows are wrong (list_condition2: rows 78, 79, 92 and 93, about 1280 px), so the open list rows do not match; the heads are 2 to 13 px off the 1:1 heads.

(3) Library fixes. `ui_layout.wrap` returns its full result together with `Overflow` (layout.e lines 394 and 501), so treating Overflow as a clipped layout in the widget runtime (two callers) is what wrap did before it reported it; no other layout kind is affected. The token edit sheets render (rest, hover, focused, wrapped with two lines, empty, one token) and token_edit_dialog is 0 px off. `focus_last_behind_modal` only acts while a modal is open and only the looks Focus drive calls it; the image combo box focus sheet shows the dotted rectangle, 15 rows, black dots on x + y odd, as the capture. Both sane.

(4) Regression sample, rendered one app at a time (at most three at once), compared by git: 1913 PNGs rewritten, all identical to the committed ones (Windows 581, Windows dark 412, Neper 412, Neper dark 412, Linux 26 and Linux dark 26 from linux_button, linux_checkbox, linux_list_box, linux_popup_menu, linux_text_edit and linux_tree_list, Cupertino 6 and Cupertino dark 6 from cup_button, cup_menu and cup_tree_list, Material 16 and Material dark 16 from material_button, _checkbox, _list, _chips, _switch and _menu, plus native_dark2, 4, 6, 7, _icb and _open for Windows dark and Neper dark). The 24 PNGs worker 49 regenerated, spot checked against their parents: windows/native_dark4 (7665 px, the list and check list box frames of the native comparison, no regression), windows/combo_hover (improved, above), native_dark_open4 and 5 (one cluster of about 750 px, the combo head text), neper/native_dark2 (tooltip text glyphs), the vs-ref composites of image_combo_box_open_art2_head and condition2_head (1000 and 1800 px), words_androidskia (127 px of glyph drift): none is a regression in a matched look. Scale (`looks_scale_check.py`, one sheet at a time, exact / within a device pixel at 1.25, 1.5, 2.0): radio_states 100 / 100 at all three, status_bar 95.2 / 100, 95.7 / 100, 95.2 / 100; both pass.

Largest gaps: the Windows light review rows (menu_bar, popup_menu, image_combo_box, combo_box, text_edit, memo, tree_list, vertical_grid, data_grid, dialog, page_control, progress_bar) and date_edit; the opt-in crisp switches; picture artwork (button icons, header icons, toolbar atlas) that is baked or a placeholder; macOS and iOS (no native capture); Windows dark (11 review, 3 started); Android (spec written references).

# Looks audit 21, 2026-10-20 (rounds 41 to 45 merged: true 1:1 v3 captures, Windows light grid, tree, check list and vertical grid retune)

Independent recompute at master 113e0749a (the shared tree carries another session's uncommitted lib/e/fmt and lib/e/js edits; I left them). New scratch comparers, not committed, none of the scripts the brief named: `a21.py` (the cells of a reference composite are its connected non background parts; each is placed in ours by the least squared difference of the old reference's cell, so a copy of the old picture still locates it; off = ours differs by more than 2 levels in any channel; glyph zone = components of strong edges 5 to 16 px tall grown 2 px; flat = a reference pixel inside a run of 11 equal pixels in a row or column with all eight neighbours also in runs), `a21ink.py` (ink boxes of word groups, agreement within 1 px), `a21m.py`, `a21c.py` and `a21e.py` (per sheet and per cell figures for the matched controls, and the rows and columns of a cell that differ across most of their length). A clean detached worktree at 113e0749a with the committed `neper-self.exe`; 70 sheet apps rendered one at a time (never the whole script), no process was killed. The only composites I rebuilt are scratch copies (scripts/looks_ref1to1.py run in the worktree for the sheets of the matched controls and the edits sheets); none is committed.

Result. One upgrade and thirteen downgrades, all on Windows light. Counts over the 30 Delphi-equivalent controls: Windows light matched 6, review 23, started 1 (66.7 %; the merged files said 18, 11, 1 and 82.7 %, audit 20's 19 had lost check list box in round 43); Windows dark matched 16, review 11, started 3 (77.3 %); Linux light and dark matched 30 (100 %); Neper light and dark ready 30 (100 %); macOS and iOS light review 20, started 10 (46.7 %), dark review 28, started 2 (57.3 %); Android light matched 8, review 18, started 4 (65.3 %), dark matched 8, review 19, started 3 (66.7 %). Overall 78.0 % (79.6 % on the merged files): Windows 72.0, Linux 100, Neper 100, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 66.0. The overlay `status.d/zzzz...-audit21.json` holds the changes.

(1) The 1:1 captures. They are real 100 % pixels. Desktop scale checked on this machine: AppliedDPI 144 (150 %), primary screen 1280 wide to a DPI unaware reader (1920 physical), the window rectangle of v3 800 x 600, so v3 is drawn at 96 dpi and stretched by DWM. Main window, old against new: a one pixel rule is one row of luma 160 between two rows of 240 in the new file and a smeared 190 over 210 over 91 in the old; vertical edge energy of data_grid_top 5.45 to 8.33, tree_list_expanded 11.2 to 14.8, vertical_grid 8.7 to 13.9; the focus rectangle is dotted. The method does not misplace the client: the frame rows and columns agree between old and new, a sub pixel shift search of the new client against the old one (both blurred equally) gives 0.25 to 0.75 px, the phase of the old double resample, and the isolated one pixel rules of the new file sit 0.24 px (sd 1.0, 42 rules) from the centroid of the same rule in the old one. The frame (title bar, borders) is still the old soft DWM grab, as the README says. Not valid for every row: 33 of the 493 captures that have an old twin come back identical to the old file in their textured pixels (every popup menu `menu-*`, `menu-item-selected-*`, `hover-menu-switch-*`, `menu-submenu-*`, and the combo drop lists `combo-box-open-list-*`, `hover-combo-list-*`): popups are separate top level windows that WM_PRINT of the owner does not draw, so they fell back to flag 2 and are still resampled. So these rows still rest on the blurred set: every popup menu and menu bar open state, the image combo box drop lists, the hint tooltips (5 of 6 sheets), the splitter live sheets, the toolbar hot sheets (not found exactly in the old captures), and all of the 231 sheets of `ref/windows` that the round 43 composites do not replace (the composites cover the grid, tree, vertical grid and check list families only; I rebuilt the sheets of the matched controls in scratch to test them, item 3).

(2) Windows light grid, tree, check list, vertical grid against `windows-1to1`. Counted = more than 2 levels off, glyphs included, per cell; in brackets the part outside the glyph zone and the flat chrome pixels. Re-renders equal the committed PNGs byte for byte (data_grid_top with its 17 arguments, data_grid, data_grid_bottom, tree_list, check_list_box and its three sheets, vertical_grid, data_grid_dialogs and the dialog state sheets); the 12 `-vs-ref` pictures differ because the committed ones were drawn against the old reference, and the `data_grid_dialogs_sort` sheet is no longer stale. Claims confirmed: data_grid_top left cell 1212 exactly (847 outside the zone, 43 flat; the right cell 1361, 383 of them the scroll bar column), hover 868, group 1643, group2 2343 (2011 of them in the 24 px footer band, 187 flat), tree_list_expanded 1032 and 1059 (2091 together, claim 2082), check_list_box_dialog 284 exactly (190, 28 and 66), states 269, hover 197, vertical_grid 1845 exactly (1295 outside the zone, 76 flat). Claims that differ: data_grid_bottom 15,049 and 13,575 (claim 15.7k; 12.7k of each is the card art strip and footer band, not drawn); data_grid_main, whose reference is the whole 1:1 window with another layout, can be measured on the collection part only: 30,092 over the 494 x 240 grid including the strip and footer, about 10.8k over the eight data rows (the claimed 11,889 is a crop of the rows); tree_list_rules is better than the note, 61 (note 3876, stale). Not retuned and far off, all captured states: the Shortcuts dialog grid 31 to 33k per cell, the Sort dialog 13.7 to 14.2k, Missing set cards 8.9k, data_grid_dialogs_sort 12.9k, the states sheet 960. Residuals: top and hover the first column's edition icons and a handful of header pixels; group the footer band; bottom the art strip; tree list the sixth Edition row's picture (the capture shows the Sixth Edition icon on its cut off last row, ours draws none, 123 px) and the Edition column text 2 px left of the capture in two ink boxes (71 of 73 ink boxes agree within 1 px; tree_list 48 of 48, tree_list_hover 49 of 49, rules 5 of 5); check list the first glyph's faint left fringe (about 8 px per row) and nothing else; vertical grid the chevrons (ours is the anti aliased `chevron_alpha`, the capture a hard edged chevron, 3 ink boxes), glyph text, and the Computed rows' pictures, which are baked. Ink boxes of the grid sheets within 1 px: top 58 of 61, hover 55 of 60, group2 18 of 21.

Stamps and fits. No pixel table of a captured region in `datagrid.e`, `gridlook.e`, `gridrules.e` or `vgridlook.e`: rules, frames, footers, group rows, focus rectangles and dotted lines are drawn rectangles. Baked pictures: every `gx_` table is a small icon (at most 399 px, the header picture 19 x 21; stars 17 x 16; check tiles 14 x 13; navigator buttons about 20 x 22), `wintreeicon` is 15 edition tiles of 22 x 16 (5280 px). The exception is `examples/looks/vertical_grid.e`: the Computed rows' artwork is baked as whole row pictures cut from the capture, 160 x 17 (colors 2880 px, legality 2720, strip 160 x 11, ellipsis, three 17 x 17 icons), about 11k px of every vertical grid cell that are exact by construction and so hide that much from the 1845; I count them as large baked regions, not evidence. Constants fitted to single captures: `Look.foot_pale` and the footer palette of 20 shades in `datagrid.e` (footer_px and shifted_px), `foot_drift` and the 0.0115, 0.010, 0.0065, 0.0055, 0.003 per px edge drift, the `gx_drift` end values (128 to 166, 145 to 181, 139 to 177, 134 to 170, 215 to 177 over 542 px), the Sort dialog's twelve dot colours, the dot parity of the tree and check lists (calibrated on the Filter list; the Conditional Formatting list agrees at 61 px), the vertical grid's hairline pair with its 0.013 px per row drift.

Rows. data_grid review: only top, hover and group (left cells) are near; the bottom, main, Shortcuts, Sort and Missing captures are not. tree_list review, the nearest of the four: one missing edition picture and two ink boxes 2 px off in the one captured expanded sheet. vertical_grid review: the chevrons are the wrong shape, the Author and shut sheets have no reference at all, and a tenth of the cell is baked. check_list_box matched (upgrade, restores the status round 43 removed): every captured state 197 to 284 px including text, 0 flat chrome px, checked boxes only in the captures, so unchecked, disabled and pressed boxes stay unverified, which the ladder allows.

(3) The matched light controls against the crisp captures. The sheets of label, checkbox, group_box, hint, image_combo_box, menu_bar, radio, radio_group, scroll_bar, splitter, status_bar, toolbar_button, list_box, button, token_edit, spin_edit and mask_edit (all their apps and arguments) re-render byte for byte as the committed PNGs. Against the 1:1 twins they do not all hold, because their 0 px figures were measured against the resampled references and the old comparers searched offsets within 2 px. The failing rows are whole one pixel edges: ours is the old blur on two rows, the capture one crisp row. Evidence: button_states first crop row 0 capture 0, ours 80, row 1 253 against 169, and ours equals the old reference to the level; token_edit bottom edge row capture 131, ours 172 then 210; menu_bar rows 1, 24 and 25 across the whole bar; list_box, image_combo_box, edit frames and status bar hover rows and columns across the whole control; group_box and radio_group frames and button borders; radio circles 1 px right in the Search dialog crop. Downgrades to review (13): button (84 of 84 crops), radio (1 of 23), radio_group (3 of 19), group_box (16 of 16), token_edit (3 of 3), image_combo_box (18 of 20), list_box (3 of 3), menu_bar (19 of 19), status_bar (rest holds, hover and report fail), toolbar_button (1 of 1), spin_edit, mask_edit, button_edit (37 of 37 crops of the edits sheets). Hold: label (7 of 7 crops, 0 flat), checkbox (29 of 29, 33 flat in all), scroll_bar (7 crops, 72 flat in all), splitter (2 crops, 20 flat), hint (1 twin crop, text differences only; the other 5 sheets are tooltips and stay on the blurred set, so hint and splitter are matched with that caveat). A row that stays matched only on grabs that are still resampled should be named before it is promoted.

(4) Regression sample, re-rendered individually and compared byte for byte (modification times checked): Linux 6 apps (linux_button, linux_checkbox, linux_list_box, linux_popup_menu, linux_text_edit, linux_tree_list), Cupertino 3 (cup_button, cup_menu, cup_tree_list), Neper dark and Windows dark 4 apps (native_dark4, native_dark6, native_dark7, native_dark_icb), Material 6 (material_button, _checkbox, _list, _chips, _switch, _menu): all equal in every Linux, Cupertino, Material, Windows dark and Neper dark file, with one hygiene finding that moves no status: `native_dark4.png` (and its 1.5x) written into `windows/`, `neper/` and `neper-dark/` differs from the committed copy there (3192 to 3780 px); its header says only windows-dark matters, and that file is identical, so the stale copies in the other folders are never claimed. Scale (`looks_scale_check.py`, exact / within a device pixel at 1.25, 1.5, 2.0): windows data_grid_top 99.5 / 99.7, 99.7 / 99.7, 100 / 100; tree_list_expanded 100 / 100 at all three. Both pass.

Largest gaps: the Windows light edges of every v3 drawn control (one pixel frames, 13 rows now review), the grid's Shortcuts, Sort, Missing set cards and bottom captures (8.9k to 33k px), popups and drop lists that have no 1:1 grab at all, macOS and iOS (no native capture), Windows dark (11 review, 3 started), Android (spec written references).
