# Looks audit 20, 2026-10-20 (rounds 39 and 40 merged: Android popup menu shortcut column, Windows light grid chrome)

Independent recompute at master f6c3fd8a5 (the shared tree carries another session's uncommitted lib/e/fmt and lib/e/js edits; I left them). New scratch comparers, not committed and none of the scripts the brief named: `a20w.py` (Windows light: ours placed against the reference sheet by the best exact-pixel count of the reference's flat pixels, over a window of 0 to 3 px or, where the sizes differ, over the whole size difference; flat = a pixel in a row or column run of 11 equal pixels, colour seen 30 times, all eight neighbours also in runs; off = ours differs by more than 2 levels in any channel; glyph zone = components of strong edges 5 to 16 px tall grown 2 px, counted separately; reference rows of one colour where ours is not uniform are our captions and are left out) and `a20m.py` (Material: reference crop placed in the committed sheet by its flat pixels outside the ink). A clean detached worktree at f6c3fd8a5 with the committed `neper-self.exe` copied in; sheet apps rendered individually through `scripts/render_looks_all.sh` with scratch TMPDIR, at most three at a time (the first parallel try made `data_grid_bottom` and `data_grid_top` fail to compile and `linux_popup_menu` fail to run, a memory effect; run alone they compile, run and equal the committed PNGs); no process was killed. Overlay `status.d/zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz-audit20.json` is empty: no upgrade, no downgrade.

Result. Both rounds hold at the status they claim. Counts over the 30 Delphi-equivalent controls: Windows light matched 19, review 10, started 1 (84.0 %); Windows dark matched 16, review 11, started 3 (77.3 %); Linux light and dark matched 30 (100 %); Neper light and dark ready 30 (100 %); macOS and iOS light review 20, started 10 (46.7 %), dark review 28, started 2 (57.3 %); Android light matched 8, review 18, started 4 (65.3 %), dark matched 8, review 19, started 3 (66.7 %). Overall 79.7 %: Windows 80.7, Linux 100, Neper 100, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 66.0. Correction to audit 19, which printed Android 64.0 and 65.3 and overall 79.5: those are audit 18's figures with the popup menu in review; round 37 put the menu back to matched and the status files (and the progress page, 65 and 67) always counted it. Two findings that move no status: the committed `data_grid_dialogs_sort` sheet is stale after round 40 (below), and `data_grid_main` has a real one pixel column error of about 120 px.

(1) Android popup menu (round 39). Reference `menu-rest` and `menu-hover` (md-menu, light and dark, 200 x 345 with a 16 px margin) placed at (14, 23) in `material/menu`, `menu_hover` and the dark twins (244 x 389). Text ink = the repository's `-textmask` PNGs (text only, more than 2 levels from the glyph-free twin): ink within 2 levels, rest and hover alike, light 99.0 % of 1957 px, dark 99.5 % of 2026 px, the claim exactly; the labels 99.0 and 99.4 %, the right aligned shortcut column 99.0 and 99.5 % (583 and 599 px), so the gap audit 19 found (83.9 and 85.0 %, 218 of 220 shortcut px off) is closed. Bit exact (0 levels) is lower: 22.0 and 30.2 % at rest, 17.1 and 23.2 % in hover; the claim is the 2 level figure. Flat px off outside the text, icons and caption row: dark 0 in both cells; light 746 in both, every one exactly 3 levels (one level beyond my tolerance): the two rim columns beside the fill (crop x 15 and 184, 297 rows each) and 152 px of the bottom rim row, the shadow tail audit 16 already accepted; px over 12 levels outside the glyph zone 0 in all four cells. Matched stands. Width, 168 against 137: the fill is 168 px wide in the reference and in ours at every body row I scanned (rows 30, 170, 300; x 16 to 183 of the crop), rest and hover, light and dark, and `manifest.csv` lists w_1x 168 too (crop width 200 = 168 + 2 x 16 margin). My scan is the longest run of the surface colour in a row; audit 19 got the same 168. No row of either image has a 137 px fill: 137 is a slip in the round 37 note ("137 px, the reference"), not a measurement, and no width error exists. The sheets re-render byte for byte.

(2) Windows light grids. Reference sheets against the committed sheets (equal to my re-renders, see below). Chrome px off (flat, text excluded) with the flat px inside the glyph zone after the slash, then the claimed flat figure (round 40 comparer) and strict figure in brackets: data_grid_main 369 / 25 (252, 205); bottom 261 / 28 (285, 122); bottom2 263 / 28 (271, 123); bottom3 137 / 14 (143, 104); top 156 / 30 (218, 84); top2 to top4 146 / 22 (208, 74); top5 218 / 35 (259, 74); hover 139 / 37 (186, 65); hover2 and hover3 138 / 26 and 138 / 22 (186, 65); group 187 / 87 (216, 64); group2 238 / 148 (268, 73); group3 156 / 82 (195, 63); sort_states 69 / 69 raw, 0 after I looked at them (every cluster sits in a header text column, y 35 to 50 and 326 to 338: our glyph stem where the capture has a pale stem, text my zone missed; the claimed 0 strict holds). All under 300 on my comparer except `data_grid_main`: 369, of which 120 px are a one pixel column at x 498, rows 241 to 395 of the collection grid (ours #EA or #F0 where the capture is white, the frame's inner right edge), so "every crop under 300" is wrong for main by that column (249 without it). The strict figures (205, 122, 84, 65, 73) are the worker's script's; my flat figures are lower than the worker's flat ones in every crop but main, and the strict script's artwork boxes cannot be reproduced without its code, so I confirm "under 300", not the strict numbers. What remains in my flat count: top and hover about 90 px at y 217 to 224 (the last data row's foot stripes, x 152 to 235 and 738 to 821, the gap the notes list) and 10 to 14 px per crop in the first header cell. Counting every pixel outside text and artwork components, not only flat ones, gives thousands per sheet (main 13,667, top 9277, bottom 29,269: v3 artwork and anti-aliased text edges my components do not cover), so these sheets are right in chrome and not in content. Other sheets, flat chrome / glyph: vertical_grid 17 / 20 (claimed 5 / 32), vertical_grid_hover 65 / 100, tree_list_expanded 28 / 350 over the whole sheet (claimed 30 / 159 per cell), tree_list 16 / 72. check_list_box_dialog, _hover and _states: 0 flat chrome and 0 in the glyph zone; counting every pixel more than 2 levels off, 21,820, 16,189 and 22,768 (text, and the capture is cut under its fourth row where ours draws the frame bottom), the same order as the worker's 16,700. The 19 matched light controls hold: I re-rendered 14 apps (label, checkbox, group_box, hint, image_combo_box, menu_bar, radio, scroll_bar, splitter, status_bar, toolbar_button, list_box, button, token_edit) plus the grid, tree, vertical grid and check list box apps; 228 Windows PNGs were rewritten and every one equals the committed file except three (next paragraph). Rules, not stamps: no `pix:` string, stamp table or pixel image in `datagrid.e`, `gridlook.e` or `gridrules.e`; the old `ring_marks` and `bar_marks` fields exist but nothing sets them. Round 40 draws one pixel rectangles (`footer_px`, `shifted_px`, `sel_dots_dialog_paint`) at positions computed from the grid's geometry (navigator position box, column x, header x), with capture colours as constants. That is drawn, but fitted: the rule's tail runs 87 and 105 px, the green footer's edge drift is 0.0115, 0.010, 0.0065, 0.0055 and 0.003 levels per pixel from the cell at x 250, the header tints and the Sort dialog's twelve dot colours are single capture values, so they are right for these captures and have no evidence beyond them. The look flag choosing the green footer picture column is `Look.foot_pale` (`gridlook.e`, default false): `examples/looks/data_grid.e` sets it for the pool and collection looks (main window capture: #BF rule, #E8EBEF and #C9E1CB, the yellow band's first picture column #E8EBEF then #FDFEE3); the bottom and top sheets leave it false and get #BC, #EBEEF2 and #C7E0C8. No other app sets it.

Stale commit. `windows/data_grid_dialogs_sort.png` (and its `-vs-ref` and 1.5x twin) is the only committed PNG that a re-render changes: 1528 px (x 13 to 324, y 90 to 109, up to 160 levels), the Sort grid's focus rectangle, which round 40's `sel_dots_dialog_paint` draws as grey dots and the committed sheet still draws as the old blue band row; the last commit of the file is 057fcdbde, before round 40, and round 40's commits do not list it. Against the reference the re-render is nearer (all px off outside text 4141 to 3202, flat chrome 15 either way). It needs regenerating; I did not (no source or PNG edits in an audit).

(3) Regression sample. Re-rendered individually and compared byte for byte with the committed files (modification times checked): Linux 26 and Linux dark 26 PNGs from linux_button, linux_checkbox, linux_list_box, linux_popup_menu, linux_text_edit and linux_tree_list; Cupertino 6 and Cupertino dark 6 from cup_button, cup_menu and cup_tree_list; Neper dark 162 and Windows dark 162 from native_dark4, native_dark6, native_dark7, native_dark_icb and the Windows apps above; Material 14 and Material dark 14 from material_button, material_checkbox, material_list, material_chips, material_switch, material_text_field and material_menu. All equal. Scale (`looks_scale_check.py`, one sheet at a time; exact / within a device pixel at 1.25, 1.5, 2.0): material:menu light 99.9 / 100, 100 / 100, 100 / 100 (dark the same); windows data_grid_top 99.6 / 99.7, 100 / 100, 100 / 100. Largest remaining gaps: macOS and iOS (no native capture, nothing matched, 47 and 57 %); Windows dark (11 review, 3 started); Android (18 and 19 review: spec-written references cannot pass, radio ring, slider, switch and tooltip text); Windows light data grid (chrome within a few hundred flat px per sheet, but v3 artwork, text and the last data row's foot stripes are not reproduced, so no sheet is pixel equal), tree list and vertical grid glyphs, page control tab art; the stale `data_grid_dialogs_sort` sheet and the `data_grid_main` right edge column.

# Looks audit 19, 2026-10-19 (rounds 37 and 38 merged: Android popup menu width, Windows light grid footers)

Independent recompute at master 48316ea1f (the commit under test; master has moved on by one macOS linker commit that touches no look). New scratch comparers, not committed and none of the scripts the brief named: `a19m.py` (Material: reference crop template-placed in the committed 1x sheet by the pixels outside the glyph zone), `a19w.py` (Windows light: flat = a pixel in a row or column run of 11 equal pixels, colour seen 30 times, not next to a non-run pixel; off = ours differs by more than 2 levels; glyph zone = components of strong edges 5 to 16 px tall, grown 2 px, counted separately), `a19c.py` (cells of a reference sheet that has blank cells). A clean worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe`; 37 sheet apps rendered one by one, three at a time, with a scratch TMPDIR (not the full `render_looks_all.sh`); no process was killed. Overlay `status.d/zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz-audit19.json` (notes and gaps of the popup menu only).

Result. No upgrade, no downgrade. Counts over the 30 Delphi-equivalent controls, unchanged from audit 18: Windows light matched 19, review 10, started 1 (84.0 %); Windows dark matched 16, review 11, started 3 (77.3 %); Linux light and dark matched 30 (100 %); Neper light and dark ready 30 (100 %); macOS and iOS light review 20, started 10 (46.7 %), dark review 28, started 2 (57.3 %); Android light matched 7, review 19, started 4 (64.0 %), dark matched 7, review 20, started 3 (65.3 %). Overall 79.5 %: Windows 80.7, Linux 100, Neper 100, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 64.7. Two findings that move no status: the Android menu text fell from 99 to 84 percent in round 37, and the committed `data_grid_bottom*` sheets are stale.

(1) Android popup menu (round 37). Reference md-menu (`menu-rest`, `menu-hover`, a real Material Web component, so the spec-written cap does not apply), light and dark, against `material/menu`, `menu_hover` and the dark twins, all four placed at the same offset. Flat reference px off outside the glyph zone: 0 in all four (the only flat px off lie in the crop's first row, where my crop meets our sheet caption); px over 12 levels outside the glyph zone: 0; the fill spans the same columns in the reference and in ours (16 to 183, 168 px at my row scan; I could not reproduce a figure of 137 px, but the extents are equal column for column), so the width fix is confirmed: exact pixels outside the text mask rose from 65,010 to 65,861 light and 66,360 to 66,638 dark against the sheet before round 37. A re-render at 48316ea1f is byte for byte the committed sheet. Honest cost: text ink (the repository's text mask, 1957 px light, 2026 dark) within 2 levels went from 99.0 and 99.5 percent before round 37 to 83.9 and 85.0 percent now. All of it is the right aligned shortcut column (x 140 to 160 of the crop; 218 of its 220 mask px differ in light): the glyphs have the right shape but sit a fraction of a pixel off, because rounding the box to whole pixels moved the column's start. The labels are about 99 percent. Status: matched holds (strict metric 0, same precedent as the slider and switch at 56 to 66 percent text); the notes now say so and carry a gap. 36 flat px of the light hover cell lie in the glyph zone (the hover state layer under text) and are counted. Other Material sheets: button, chips, list, dialog, radio, tabs, slider, text field (and the menu) re-rendered, 40 PNGs in both schemes, all equal to the committed files.

(2) Windows light grids (round 38). Whole sheets against `ref/windows`, ours placed where the sizes differ; chrome = flat reference px off outside the glyph zone, with the sheet's own caption rows excluded (our captions sit on the reference's blank margin), glyph-zone flat px counted next to it. data_grid_main 547 chrome, 35 glyph (before round 38 on the same comparer 755 and 35: the footer fixes are real, direction and size agree with the worker's 553 to 381); data_grid_top 327 / 6; data_grid_hover 378 / 12; data_grid_group2 218 / 228; data_grid_bottom 757 / 76 (the committed sheet gives 649, see below); data_grid_sort_states 129 / 9; vertical_grid 37 / 0 and vertical_grid_hover 165 / 0 (every one of those flat px is a coloured ClearType fringe pixel at a glyph edge, so no chrome px are off; but the text zone is only 66 percent exact, glyph px over 12 levels 479 and 2395, and 11,143 and 55,525 non-flat px differ in total, the narrower text of the worker's gap); tree_list_expanded 322 / 56 and 1290 glyph-zone px, 66.6 percent of text ink exact. My figures are higher than the worker's (main 381, top 231, hover 212, group2 158, bottom 282, sort 0, tree 30 per cell) because they are whole sheet and unmasked (v3 toolbar artwork) and use another flat definition; the order and the direction are the same. No data grid sheet reaches 0. Check list box: the dialog sheet 0 chrome px off, 2735 glyph-zone flat px and 21,820 px over 2 levels in the content rows (the soft ClearType of v3 against ours); the states and hover references are truncated (their last three rows are bare background; excluded) and give 0 chrome, 22,768 and 16,189 differing px in the content rows, none of them flat. Status: data grid, tree list and vertical grid stay review; check list box stays matched; no promotion, as the worker said. Stale sheets: a fresh render of `data_grid_bottom` (deterministic, run twice) differs from the committed `data_grid_bottom`, `2`, `3` (and their 1.5x and `-vs-ref` files, nine files) by 120, 120 and 60 px, at most 3 levels, in the footer rows y 228 to 247 (x 24 to 612): the committed files were rendered before the last footer commit. My chrome count is 649 on the committed sheet and 757 on the fresh one, so the fresh render is slightly farther from the reference. Not committed here; whoever commits the next render should expect them. The matched light controls hold: button, label, checkbox, radio group, group box, list box, scroll bar, hint, status bar, splitter and check list box (11 of the 19, all their sheets and states) re-rendered byte for byte equal to the committed PNGs, 237 Windows light files rewritten, nine different (all `data_grid_bottom*`).

# Looks audit 18, 2026-10-19 (round 35 merged: Android text model, Windows dark group box and header)

Independent recompute at master e4ea5ea11 (c2cf5d4b4 plus the progress page commit; a css commit by another session landed on master meanwhile and touches no look). Own scratch comparers (not committed; none of the scripts the brief named): `tm2.py` (Material), `pcmp3.py` and `probe.mjs` (the text probe), `wd.py` (Windows native), `wl3.py` (Windows light bands), `lm.mjs` (line metrics in Chromium). Two clean worktrees with the committed `neper-self.exe`; every sheet app (175) rendered through `scripts/render_looks_all.sh` with a scratch TMPDIR, at most three at a time (four at a time ran out of process slots and failed compiles, which I re-ran); no process was killed. Overlay `status.d/zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz-audit18.json`.

Result. One downgrade, no upgrade: the Android popup menu (light and dark) goes from matched to review, because since round 35 the menu is one pixel narrower than the reference. Counts over the 30 Delphi-equivalent controls: Windows light matched 19, review 10, started 1 (84.0 %); Windows dark matched 16, review 11, started 3 (77.3 %); Linux light and dark matched 30 (100 %); Neper light and dark ready 30 (100 %); macOS and iOS light review 20, started 10 (46.7 %), dark review 28, started 2 (57.3 %); Android light matched 7, review 19, started 4 (64.0 %, was 65.3), dark matched 7, review 20, started 3 (65.3 %, was 66.7). Platform idiom rows (16): Android light matched 4 (switch, slider, chip, fab), dark 3. Overall 79.5 % (was 79.7): Windows 80.7, Linux 100, Neper 100, macOS and iOS 52.0, Android 64.7.

(1) Android text model. (a) Origin: `capture.mjs` launches `chrome-headless-shell-win64` with `--disable-lcd-text --font-render-hinting=none`; that binary here is Google Chrome for Testing 149.0.7827.55 on Windows 11, Roboto read from `C:/Windows/Fonts`. I re-ran `capture.mjs` for four cells (button-text-rest and list-item-one-rest, light and dark): the PNGs are byte for byte the committed ones. In a sample of 9535 text mask pixels, 96 percent lie on the line between the surface and the ink colour (grey anti-aliasing, no colour fringes). So the claim holds: Skia over DirectWrite on Windows, grey. It is not an Android device (Android's Skia rasterises with FreeType and its own hinting and gamma policy). Every Material status now means "matches Material Web in Chrome on Windows", not "looks like Android text"; the notes should say so.

(b) Measured (`tm2.py`: each 1x reference crop trimmed to the component plus 6 px, template-placed in the committed 1x sheet, first by the pixels outside the text, then the candidates by the number of pixels over 2 levels in the whole crop; exact = every channel within 2 levels). With the repository's text-mask PNGs, text ink pixels exact, light then dark: button 97.0 and 97.7 (6292 and 6357 px), text field 97.0 and 96.8 (20,716 and 21,469), chips 93.6 and 96.1 (14,451 and 14,830), list 98.9 and 99.2, combo box 97.9 and 97.9, tabs 99.2 and 99.4, app bar 97.9 and 98.6, menu 99.0 and 99.5, snackbar 95.0 and 94.8, navigation bar 93.1 and 93.6, date picker 93.0 and 93.5, search bar 98.5 and 98.9, fab 99.3 and 99.8, badge 100 and 100. The weak ones: slider 55.6 and 41.9 (198 px, the value label), switch 65.8 and 39.4 (73 px, icon labels), progress 75.5 and 81.8 (188 and 176 px, the buffer label), tooltip 1 to 9 and 3 (the tooltips sit on a fractional x in the reference; the rich title colour differs). Totals over the placed cells: light 95.8 % of 94,036 px (191 cells; dialog 99.0 and date picker 93.0 placed separately), dark 93.0 % of 105,692 px (193 cells). The worker's 93.4 and 93.9 are therefore reproduced (mine is higher in light because my comparer placed one of the two tooltip cells). With my own mask (reference pixels more than 12 levels from the cell's text-free twin, which also takes the strong anti-aliased icon edges) the totals are 90.2 % light and 89.1 % dark; with the text mask grown by one pixel 97.1 and 94.8. Not an inflated figure.

(c) Principled or fitted. `dwtext.e` is a model of a fixed renderer: hinted TrueType outline, six by five samples, three ClearType channels as boxes of six sample columns, five vertical weights, the mean of the channels through Skia's own pre-blend table keyed by the colour. Its constants were measured on isolated glyphs from the same Chromium (`dwprobe.py`), not on the gallery, and no constant is per control or per reference. Test on something no reference uses: a Neper sheet (`zprobe`, a scratch app, not committed) with Roboto at 13, 15, 17, 18 and 19 px, weights 400 and 500 (the reference set has 11, 12, 14, 16, 22 and up), seven colours no reference has (luminance tables 1, 2, 3 and 4 of Skia's eight), on both surfaces, against a fresh Chromium 149 capture of the same string at the same pixel. With the text starting on a whole pixel: 98.6 % of ink pixels within 2 levels in light, 99.3 % in dark (cells 98.0 to 99.1 and 98.7 to 99.7; over 99.6 % within 12 levels). That is better than the references because the references carry control and layout differences. So the model generalises; the 93 to 97 percent in the references is not the rasteriser. When I centred the same text in a 320 px cell instead, the match fell to 48 % light and 51 % dark, but a sweep of the Chromium start over sixteenths of a pixel returns 92.6 to 98.0 % at the right phase, and the phase that fits is one minus the fraction of the start Blink computes ((320 minus the width) over 2: 0.148, 0.914, 0.039, 0.406, 0.688, 0.445 for the six cells, measured 0.125, 0.938, 0.063, 0.438, 0.688, 0.438). That is the sheet harness putting a text box on a whole pixel in its cell (the looks5 comment in `label.e`), not the text model. It does mean the sheets cannot test centred text at a fractional start; the app bar title (97.9 %) is the only reference that exercises one.

(d) Line boxes and 64ths. In Chromium here, Roboto Regular and Medium, 8 to 40 px, nine line heights each (594 cases, the baseline from a zero-height inline-block, the line height from the box): the rule "round the OS/2 usWinAscent and usWinDescent of the pixel size, floor the half leading" has 0 misses; the hhea metrics (the rule before the commit) 190. Widths of 30 strings at ten sizes are all multiples of 1/64 px. The claim is right, and `layout.e` implements it behind `chromium_lines` (Android only; the other looks re-render byte for byte, see (4)).

(e) Statuses. I recomputed the flat and ring figures of audit 17 (flat = 3 x 3 spread of at most 6 levels, ink margin 2 px) on the sheets of 113b678bf and of now: outside the text no control got worse except the popup menu, and a few got better (light flat px off and px over 12 levels, old to new: button 270/883 to 12/584 with the icon button padding fix, snackbar 65/149 to 54/142, badge 0/153 to 0/127; unchanged: chips 134/579 to 134/580, combo box 24/574, text field 0/200, list 0/0, fab 8/217 to 8/220, page control 0/40, progress 0/8, switch 0/244, slider 50/323; dark the same pattern, button 152/1205 to 0/787). The eight matched Delphi controls with the new text: button, text edit, combo box, list box, page control and dialog hold (text 97 to 99 %, flat 0 to 12); progress bar holds on its shape (0 flat, 8 px over 12 levels; its 188 px of label text 76 to 82 % exact, a known control difference); the popup menu does not. Popup menu: text 99.0 % light and 99.5 % dark and flat 0, but the fill is 136 px wide against the reference 137 (it was 137 at audit 17), so the right edge column is off in all rows: 301 px over 12 levels per cell in light (0 before), 311 rows over 2 levels in dark (57 px over 12 in the hover cell, 1 in rest). A shape error over the full height, the rule that downgraded the badge in audit 17: review in both schemes; one column of width fixes it. The four rows matched without a Delphi counterpart hold: chip (text 93.6 and 96.1, light flat 134 in the elevated hover shadows as in audit 17, dark 0), fab (99.3 and 99.8, flat 8 and 0), switch (0 flat; 73 px of icon text 66 and 39 % exact), slider light (value label 56 % of 198 px; my comparer cannot tell the labelled states apart, so its 50 flat px are not reliable and equal the old figure). The spec-written references (snackbar, tooltip, navigation bar, top app bar, search bar, badge, date picker) stay at review, as the policy of audits 15 and 16 says; with the new text their numbers are search bar 98.5, app bar 97.9, badge 100, snackbar 95, navigation bar 93.1, date picker 93.0, which says the CSS and our render agree, nothing about the platform. The Android notes still carry the gap "text edges are not pixel equal ... 45,583 of 69,663" and the round 31 wording: out of date after round 35; only the popup menu entries are rewritten here.

(2) Windows dark, own comparer (`wd.py`: each 1x native dark capture, whole image, best placement by exact pixel count in the committed 1x dark sheet, off = any channel over 2 levels, glyphs counted). Group box: `groupbox-rest` and `groupbox-disabled` against `native_dark6`: 0 px off each (largest difference 2 levels): matched, confirmed. Header: `x-header-itemsview-rest`, `-hot` and `-pressed` against `native_dark7`: 0 px off; `-sort-up`: 21 px (x 126 to 134, y 8 to 12, up to 121 levels: the sort arrow): confirmed. The other real dark header captures are not reproduced: `x-header-explorer-*` 5386 to 5403 px off, `listview-header-hot` 13,816 and `-pressed` 18,328; the data grid stays review (the derived grid sheets, trees and dialog are untouched). Precedent and a flag: this is how list box was matched (a sheet in native geometry, audit 17), so group box is matched on the same terms, but the group box the apps draw (`group_box.png`, the dialogs) is still the derived muted frame, the native caption is black on #202020, and the overlay's gaps still say "status stays started" next to a matched status. At 1.5x neither native sheet matches its 1.5x capture (group box 1252 px, header 1144 to 1175 px, and list box 4546 px, so 1x is the convention, as it was). The five other derived rows stay review: progress bar, toolbar button, status bar, page control, menu bar; their sheets are byte equal to audit 17 and their statuses are review. Windows dark counts: matched 16 (label, button, check box, radio, text edit, spin edit, combo box, image combo box, mask edit, memo, group box, splitter, popup menu, hint, scroll bar, list box), review 11, started 3: 77.3 % (audit 17: 76 %).

(3) Windows light. Between 113b678bf and now the only Windows light PNGs that changed are four new sheets (`native_dark6` and `native_dark7`, 1x and 1.5x); the ClearType code did not change, and the Android changes are gated to the Android profile. All 937 Windows light PNGs re-rendered from a clean worktree equal the committed ones. So the 19 matched rows hold; I did not re-measure them against the v3 captures with audit 17's comparer. A different, rougher spot check (`wl3.py`: each reference sheet cut into pieces, each piece placed within our sheet; flat reference px over 2 levels outside the 2 px ink margin, glyph zone counted separately): button 0 flat off (1486 glyph-zone px of 25,322), button_states 0, button_hover 0, edits_hover 0, edits_dialog 0, combo_box_edit 0, combo_box_dialog 0, list_box_dialog 0, image_combo_box_dialog 0; checkbox_dialog 18, group_box_dialog 2, checkbox_states 61, edits_states 132, radio_group_dialog 186, button_kinds 197 (near-identical pieces make my placement ambiguous there); check_list_box_states 3131 and dialog 154,319 are review rows. Not the metric of earlier audits; no status moves.

(4) Re-renders. The full run at e4ea5ea11 (every file rewritten, checked by modification time) leaves every Linux (103), Linux dark (103), Cupertino (78), Cupertino dark (78), Neper (636), Neper dark (620), Windows (937), Windows dark (but six files), Material (90) and Material dark (90) PNG equal to the committed one, including the six Linux, three Cupertino and four Neper dark sheets of the brief. The six are `windows-dark/native_dark_open0` to `native_dark_open5` at 1.5x, one pixel each at (61, 169), 40 levels, as in audit 17; it came out the same in both worktrees, so it is deterministic, not a race: the committed files are one pixel stale against the source. Not committed.

(5) Scale (`looks_scale_check.py`, one sheet at a time; exact / within a device pixel at 1.25, 1.5, 2.0; all pass): Material chips light 97.2 / 100, 97.5 / 100, 98.4 / 100 (dark 95.7, 97.1, 98.3); list light 99.9 / 100, 99.8 / 100, 99.9 / 100 (dark 99.9, 99.9, 100); button light 97.7 / 100, 100 / 100, 100 / 100 (dark 98.0, 100, 99.9); Windows button_states 83.6 / 100, 79.0 / 100, 98.1 / 100; edits_hover 100 / 100 at all three.

Largest remaining gaps: macOS and iOS (no native capture, 47 and 57 %, nothing matched); Windows dark (11 review, 3 started: the derived progress bar, toolbar, status bar, page control and menu bar rows that natively look otherwise, the explorer and list view header captures, trees, grids, dialog, and 1.5x captures that no sheet reproduces); Android (19 and 20 review: the spec-written references cannot pass, the radio ring, the popup menu width, slider, switch and tooltip text); Windows light data grid, tree list and vertical grid (v3 artwork), page control tab art, popup menu logo.

# Looks audit 17, 2026-10-19 (round 33 merged: Windows dark, Windows light, ClearType, Android)

Independent recompute at master 113b678bf with new scratch comparers (not committed; none of the scripts the brief named). Every sheet was rendered in a clean worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe` (all 173 apps through `scripts/render_looks_all.sh`, scratch TMPDIR); no process was killed. Overlay `status.d/zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz-audit17.json`.

Result. Nine downgrades, no upgrade. Windows dark: progress bar, toolbar button, status bar, page control, group box and menu bar go from matched to review. Android (light and dark): search bar, top app bar and badge go from matched to review. Counts over the 30 Delphi-equivalent controls: Windows light matched 19, review 10, started 1 (84 %); Windows dark matched 15, review 12, started 3 (76 %, claimed 21 matched, 84 %); Linux light and dark matched 30 (100 %); Neper light and dark ready 30 (100 %); macOS and iOS light review 20, started 10 (47 %), dark review 28, started 2 (57 %), no matched; Android light matched 8, review 18, started 4 (65 %), dark matched 8, review 19, started 3 (67 %). Platform idiom rows (16): Android light matched 4 (was 7), dark 3 (was 6), the three downgrades. Overall 79.6 % (claimed 80.4): Windows 80, Linux 100, Neper 100, macOS and iOS 52, Android 66.

(1) Windows dark, own comparer (`wc.py`: each native 1x dark capture template-placed in the committed 1x dark sheets, every pixel, off = any channel over 2 levels; structure = outside 2 px of ink). List box: the five `listbox-*` captures (130 x 150) against `native_dark4` are 2 px off counted, 0 structure, in the scroll column; the committed sheets equal the re-render. Image combo box: the seven `combo-list-*` captures against `native_dark_icb` and `native_dark_open2` to 5: 0 px off in the five head captures, 4 px counted and 0 structure in the two open lists. Both matched, confirmed. Caveats: the sheet's `picture` cell (the check icon) has no native capture, and the new `x-tooltip-wrapped` capture has no cell in any sheet (the hint sheet shows only the one-line tooltip; the best placement finds 4744 px off), so the hint is matched with one captured state not drawn; left, flagged. Dialog: `dialog-active` 1224 px counted against `dialog_native`, 0 structure; the message box and task dialog captures have no sheet: stays review.

Rule applied to the derived rows. A derived row stays matched only where no capture of its control exists. A derived row for which a real native dark capture exists, and which does not reproduce it, is review, whatever the reason: a native control that stays light under DarkMode, or an unthemed light frame, is still what Windows draws, and a dark design that differs from it is a choice, not a match. Measured, counted px off at the best placement: progress bar 2004 to 3969 of 9240 per capture (x-progress-explorer, progress-*), toolbar 1220 to 4870 of 16,640 (hot and checked fills stay light natively), status bar 7124 to 7320 of 18,000, page control 23,140 to 23,333 of 27,600, group box 1670 of 18,000 (native frame #DCDCDC with a black caption, ours a muted frame; the round 15 note that no usable reference exists was about readability, the captures exist), menu bar thousands (the native bar is white with black text). All six go to review. The splitter has no capture at all: it stays matched, flagged derived and unmeasured, on the maintainer's 2026-10-17 acceptance (if that is not counted, Windows dark is 14 matched and 74 %). The matched rows with real captures hold: check box, label, radio, text edit, memo, popup menu, scroll bar, button, hint. Check list box (4205 to 5992 counted against the dark list view captures), tree list (27,980 counted of 30,000 against x-tree-checkboxes), data grid header (5424 counted against x-header-itemsview-rest) and date edit (46,161 of 58,320 against datepicker-open) are far from native and stay review or started. The `darkx` gallery adds 100 PNGs (50 captures at two scales). The stamped dark frame and bar marks of the data grid are gone.

(2) Windows light. `docs/ui/looks/marks` no longer exists, no example app or library file reads a marks file, and `dialog_marks` is gone. The dialog grid chrome is drawn from rules in `data_grid_dialog_states.e`, with measured hot and pressed colours as constants (a fitted palette, not a stamp). Band comparer (`lc.py`: reference pieces cut at gap 6, each 24 x 120 band placed within 8 px of its piece, flat = runs of 11 with the colour 30 or more times, glyphs counted and excluded within 2 px of ink): popup menu, 76 sheets, 301 flat px counted and 35 excluded (7 in each card art crop, worst 33 counted in popup_menu_sel_cardart_1; the Search menu now draws v3's bitmaps: 5 counted, 0 excluded); page control, 21 sheets, 1778 counted, 187 excluded, the Statistics strips 107 to 129 counted and 14 excluded each; data grid, 34 sheets, still 17 to 1400 excluded px per crop (sort 1346 counted and 25 excluded, missing_states 1724 and 780, shortcuts_states 3986 and 647); tree list rest sheet 357 counted and 6 excluded, expanded 2588 and 1189, hover 1955 and 1137; vertical grid 288 and 22, hover 4178 and 2733. On multi-cell sheets my figures are upper bounds: our sheets carry caption labels the references do not, so my placement does not reproduce the worker's per-crop chrome numbers (tree_list 3 / 85, hover 0 / 264) and I did not confirm them. No status moves: data grid, tree list and vertical grid stay review; page control stays review (strips close, v3 tab art baked); popup menu is the nearest to matched (35 flat px outside ink, all in the card art menus, plus the eBay logo) and stays review. The 19 matched light controls hold: the 1x sheets of the 18 that round 33 did not touch are byte for byte the audit 16 sheets (`git diff 6d9be31dd 113b678bf` lists none), label alone changed (ClearType) and measures 18 px counted, 0 excluded; my comparer on the rest (counted / excluded): checkbox 426 / 0, group box 38 / 17, hint 25 / 0, image combo box 799 / 11, list box 333 / 2, radio group 375 / 0, scroll bar 4 / 0, splitter 3 / 1, status bar 54 / 6, token edit 0 / 0, toolbar button 0 / 0, button 1361 / 45. Check list box and menu bar come out large (18,152 and 1754 excluded) because their reference sheets are other layouts of v3 dialog crops and my placement fails there; I rely on the byte-identical sheets for them.

(3) ClearType, own comparison (`cmpct.py`: the fixture built with the committed compiler, `--dump`, each 16 x 14 cell of our black-on-white atlas against the oracle PNG, exact equality of all channels, empty oracle cells skipped): 5105 of 5320 identical, 95.96 %. The worker's 96.47 % is the same 5105 identical cells over 5292: the denominator dropped one cell per atlas (audit 16 used 5320 and reported 94.79 %), so like for like the gain is 94.79 to 95.96 %, 1.17 points, not 1.68. By face: Segoe UI regular 96.4, bold 97.7, Tahoma regular 93.9, bold 95.2. By size, Segoe UI regular 11 95.8, 12 99.5, 13 98.4, 14 98.4, 15 98.4, 16 99.5, 18 98.9, 24 82.1; bold 11 98.4, 12 99.5, 13 98.9, 14 98.4, 15 98.9, 16 98.9, 18 99.5, 24 88.9; Tahoma regular 11 94.7, 12 95.3, 13 93.7, 14 97.4, 16 94.2, 21 87.9; bold 11 95.3, 12 95.3, 13 96.3, 14 93.7, 16 96.8, 21 93.7. The fixture's regression check (no arguments) exits 0. `NEPER_TEXT_AA=grayscale`: the button, button_kinds and label sheets of Windows and Windows dark, 1x and 1.5x (12 files), rendered at 113b678bf with the variable set equal the same sheets rendered at 3e51130e9^ (before ClearType), 0 px differ.

(4) Android, own comparer (`mc.py`: each reference crop trimmed to the component plus 6 px, template-placed in the committed 1x sheet with the text ink masked in the search, tie broken by the full crop; flat = 3 x 3 spread of at most 6 levels; ink margin 2 px). Search bar (five cells, both schemes) and top app bar (four cells): 0 flat px off and 0 px over 12 levels outside the ink margin, confirmed; but 19 to 40 percent of the search bar's text ink pixels and 40 to 47 percent of the app bar's differ by more than 12 levels (rms 10 to 23), so the text is not pixel equal and the claim of glyph pixels counted 0 is true only because the flat metric does not see antialiased glyph edges. Badge: flat 0 off outside ink, 5 to 7 counted, but 137 to 146 px over 12 levels outside ink across six cells (up to 35 in one), where round 33 reports 0 ring. Chip: dark 0 flat off in 45 cells; light 0 except the two elevated hover cells (49 and 83 flat px, shadow); the input chip's remove icon is 21 to 29 px over 12 levels (was 83 to 101): the fix is real, matched holds. Radio: 0 flat off, 10 to 25 px over 12 levels per cell (was 64 to 75): still review. Snackbar 47 to 53 flat px off, navigation bar 12, date picker (dark) 575 over 12 levels: radio, hint, snackbar, nav bar and date picker stay review.

Reference policy. `scripts/material/gallery.html` uses only these Material Web elements: checkbox, chip-set, circular and linear progress, dialog, divider, elevation, fab, icon-button, list, menu, primary-tab, radio, select-option, slider, switch, tabs, text-button. The snackbar, tooltip, navigation bar, top app bar, search bar, badge and date picker are plain CSS written from the M3 specification (the file's own header says so). Round 33 matched three of them without saying the reference is spec-written; the policy (audits 15 and 16) caps these at review, so search bar, top app bar and badge are downgraded in both schemes. Their numbers show that the CSS and our render agree, which is evidence about our reading of the spec, not about the platform.

Blink line metrics. The commit text says ascent rounded up and descent rounded down, but `lib/e/text/layout.e` takes `ceil` of both the ascent and the descent magnitude (FreeType's size metrics: ascender up, the negative descender down) and floors the half leading. Measured on the references (last x-height row of the ink, from the -notext twin): the 22 px app bar title in its 28 px line box has its baseline 21 px under the box top (ceil: 21 + 6 = 27, half leading 0, baseline 21), the 16 px search text in a 24 px box 17 (ceil: 15 + 4, half leading 2), the 14 px snackbar action in a 20 px box 14 (ceil: 13 + 4, half leading 1). The coded rule reproduces all three. Plain rounding (A 20, D 5 at 22 px) also reaches 21 and 17, and Blink's round-then-borrow rule can too, so these three sizes cannot separate the candidates; but the rule is a rule, not a per-control offset, and fits what was measured. The commit message wording is misleading, the code is right.

Polygon corners. A 20 px radio ring (reference radio-unchecked-rest, 2 px stroke) against a 8 x supersampled model: true circle error sum 2288 (79 px over 12 levels), 8-gon 7350, 12-gon 2467, 16-gon 1077 (33 px), 20-gon 1332, 24-gon 1560, 32-gon 1821, a 16-gon rotated half a step 1892. The minimum is the 16-gon with vertices on the circle at multiples of 45 degrees, as claimed, so the polygon is a real property of Chromium's output at 20 px; the exponent 1.2 on coverage (error 834, 21 px) is a fitted constant, and I tested only this radius.

Text fit. `c18c66b3a` applies a 3-tap horizontal spread of 7 % (nothing below 3 %), a coverage power near 1 for dark text and 0.65 for light text keyed on luminance, and quarter pixel pen positions. Skia has luminance-keyed contrast and gamma tables and subpixel glyph positions, so the form of the model is principled, but the constants (7 %, 3 %, 0.65, quarter pixel) were fitted on the Android reference crops, as the commit says: the text is tuned toward the references, not derived from Skia's tables, and it still leaves 19 to 67 percent of text ink pixels more than 12 levels off. It lowers rms (chips 15 to 12 light) and is not an exact model. It is gated to the Android profile; every other look re-renders byte for byte.

(5) Re-renders. The full run at 113b678bf leaves every Linux, Linux dark, Cupertino, Cupertino dark, Neper, Neper dark, Windows, Windows dark (but six files), Material and Material dark PNG equal to the committed file, including the six Linux, three Cupertino and four Neper dark sheets named in the brief; the Neper and Neper dark Windows replica cells changed by the ClearType merge are committed and equal. The six that differ are `windows-dark/native_dark_open0` to `native_dark_open5` at 1.5x: one pixel each at (61, 169), 40 levels, too small to call a regression (a stale or racing write of one cell label pixel); not committed.

(6) Scale (`looks_scale_check.py`, one sheet at a time; exact / within a device pixel at 1.25, 1.5, 2.0; all pass): Windows data_grid_dialogs_sort 100 / 100 at all three; page_control_stats_5 99.6 / 100, 98.8 / 100, 92.4 / 100; popup_menu_sel_cardart_1 100 / 100 at all three; Material search_bar light 99.6 / 100, 99.7 / 100, 100 / 100 (dark 99.6, 99.6, 100); radio light 96.5 / 100, 100 / 100, 100 / 100 (dark 96.0, 100, 100).

Largest remaining gaps: macOS and iOS (no capture, 47 and 57 %); Windows dark (12 review, 3 started: six derived rows now measured against captures they deliberately differ from, trees, grids, date edit, dialog); Android (review 18 and 19; the spec-written references cap the search bar, top app bar, badge, snackbar, navigation bar and date picker; radio ring, text edges); Windows light data grid, tree and vertical grid (v3 artwork, dialog grid chrome still hundreds of px per crop), page control tab art, popup menu logo; ClearType 4 % of oracle glyphs still different (Segoe UI 24 px 82 %, Tahoma 21 px 88 %).

(1) Windows dark, own comparer (`wcmp.py`: each 1x native dark capture cut to its control rectangle plus 3 px, template-searched in all 327 committed 1x dark sheets, every pixel compared, off = any channel more than 2 levels; glyph-excluded = outside 2 px of ink, ink = more than 60 levels from the crop's modal colour; glyph-counted = nothing set aside). Independent recompute at master 8efa05dec with new scratch comparers (not committed; none of the scripts the brief named). Renders in a clean worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe` copied in; no process was killed. Overlay `status.d/zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz-audit15.json`.

Result. Windows light matched 19, review 10, started 1 (84 %); Windows dark matched 13, review 13, started 4 (72 %, was matched 10, review 16); Linux light and dark matched 30 of 30; Neper light and dark ready 30; macOS and iOS unchanged (light review 28, started 11; dark review 36, started 3); Android light matched 8, review 28, started 4 (61 %, was 12 matched) and Android dark matched 7, review 30, started 3 (63 %, was 11). Downgrades: Android chip, tabs (page control), popup menu, list box, both schemes. No Windows control that was matched regressed.

(1) Material, own comparer (each reference crop template-searched in the committed 1x sheet on its glyph-free twin, best placement within 3 px of the centred one; reference flat pixel = 3 x 3 spread of at most 6 levels, off = ours differs by more than 2 levels, glyph pixels = within 2 px of the reference's own ink, 2 px lines and rims reported separately). Exact (0 flat px off beyond the ink margin, 0 ring px over 12 levels, light and dark): checkbox, radio, text field, dialog, dialog icon, app bar, button (12 px at 3 levels in the light elevated hover shadow tail only), switch (3 px of the icon disc), fab (the large FAB is now exact; the extended FAB's right edge column is 27 ring px at up to 20 levels in its four cells, light only), icon buttons including the outlined focus ring with the primary outline, combo box (10 ring px at the arrow tip in focus, disabled and error), progress (6 and 4 px at the partial circular arc ends), slider light. Not exact: chip (input chip remove icon 18 to 26 px off and 12 to 14 ring px in every state; selected input chip light 148, 79, 79, 71 px), tabs (primary icons cell: the reference draws a star icon, ours an image icon, 22 px; matglyph has no star), popup menu (62 px per scheme: the Search row text is 4 px left of the reference and its shortcut 5 px right, its icon is 15 px wide against 17; Settings and Refresh draw a gear and a turn arrow where the reference draws star and plus placeholders), list (two-line rows 51 px each in rest and disabled), slider dark (pressed 27 px, hover 6), and the controls outside the claimed list: navigation bar, search bar (5 to 338 px), snackbar (4 to 41 px).

Audit 14 findings. The radio ring is still about 8 percent heavy (ring coverage 20,418 against 18,936 in unchecked rest; mean 11 levels on the ring pixels, 40 percent of them more than 12 levels off), so radio stays review. Checkbox hover, focus and pressed rings are now on-surface and exact (unchecked rest 0.4 levels mean on the ring), but the checked mark differs (about 23 px over 40 levels, up to 160, per checked cell), so checkbox stays review. The outlined icon-button ring is resolved (0 off, 0 ring in all four variants and six states, both schemes), but the settings gear in icon-button-outlined-pressed (light) is 181 px over 40 levels and a third of all icon ink differs by more than 2 levels, so the toolbar button stays review. The text-ink exactness claim is still false: ink pixels differing by more than 2 levels, light: dialog 6575 of 7344 (the audit 14 figure, unchanged), button 6256 of 6755, text field 19,914 of 21,204, list 9953 of 10,494, combo box 7275 of 7528, chips 13,631 of 14,671 (89 to 97 percent in dark too). Total ink is right (ratio 0.94 to 1.0), but lists and fields sit one row off.

Reference corrections, checked against the M3 token set and Material Web (tokens/_md-comp-outlined-button.scss lists focus-outline-color among its unsupported tokens, so the M3 token exists and Material Web does not implement it). Focused outlined button and icon button with a primary outline: defensible, it is the M3 token. Large FAB icon 36 dp: defensible (the spec and Material Web's own large icon size are 36; the 24 came from the gallery's `svg.i` rule). Input chip without a state disc on its remove button: defensible (the capture forced every ripple's hover; the focus stands on the chip's own action). Rich tooltip level 2: matches the M3 rich tooltip container elevation. No-icon app bar title at 16 dp: matches the M3 small top app bar, but the app bar reference is plain CSS written from the spec, not a Material Web capture. List pressed frame as the settled 10 percent layer: the spec state layer, where a Material Web press is an animated ripple. None of the five is tuned toward our output beyond what the specification supports. One caveat: the gallery's icons are the legacy Material Icons paths picked by its author (`gen_glyphs.py` reads them from `gallery.html`; Material Web ships no icons), so icon shapes are matched to a hand-picked reference, and the gallery uses star and plus placeholders for Settings, Refresh and the middle tab, which our look now answers with real gear, turn and image icons: those cells cannot match.

(2) The 12 Material PNGs that a post-merge render changed (image_combo_box, menu, menu_hover, light and dark, 1x and 1.5x) are stale commits, not nondeterminism. (3) Windows. One discrepancy: the fixture's own report says the ClearType hinted points equal GDI's for 20 of 12082 glyphs where the README says 10,566; the pixels match the README, so the points line looks mis-scoped, not the pixels.

Windows light, 275 reference sheets cut into pieces (XY cut, gap 6, placed in the committed sheet within 3 px, reference flat = equal-colour runs of 11 or more with the 8 neighbours in runs and the colour 30 or more times; v3 coloured artwork components up to 30 x 30 grown by 2 excluded; glyph pixels within 2 px of ink excluded / counted). All 19 matched controls hold: label, check list box, checkbox, group box, hint, image combo box, menu bar, radio, radio group, scroll bar, splitter, status bar, token edit and toolbar button at 0 / 0; list box 0 / 3; button 16 / 22 (audit 9: 18 / 19; worst button_kinds 13). # Looks audit 14, 2026-10-19 (rounds 25 and 26: Linux, Neper dark, Android, Cupertino)

Downgrades: Android checkbox, radio, toolbar button (both schemes).

(1) Linux. Overlay bug: `round26-ld` replaced the toolbar button and popup menu entries with a notes-only entry, and an overlay entry replaces the base wholesale, so both fell to planned (popup menu had been matched, toolbar button review): Linux showed 28 matched and 18 planned. Restored by this overlay. Dialog: independently recaptured with `scripts/linux/gallery.py` in WSL (Xvfb) at the true 560 x 420 window: byte for byte equal to the committed reference in both schemes. The pre-fix reference (23dbb915f^) was 550 x 410, and the new capture squeezed to that size matches it at 0.30 mean level against 2.9 for a plain crop, so the old reference was a squeezed picture. Our dialog against the recapture (placement 42,22): text ink px differing by more than 2 levels 4 of 1731 light, 0 of 1715 dark; heading, body and button rows max 2 levels; card and shadow strict 54 px at 7 levels (light), 2 px at 3 (dark); flat 8 light, 0 dark. Matched confirmed.
Full comparer, 178 crops per scheme (reference on the window colour, flat = 8 neighbours within 2 levels, ink = more than 60 levels from the crop's two dominant colours, grown 2 px): flat px off 0 in every control and scheme except dialog light (8, card corners). Strict outside ink: button 24, checkbox 9 and 0, combo box 20 and 25, data grid 32 and 34, text edit 40 and 57, progress bar 13 and 28, mask edit 6 and 8, hint dark 34 (all at most 9 levels: ring corners, arrows); every other control 0 to 2. Glyphs (reference ink px differing, light): button 28 of 3109, checkbox 10 of 2874, combo box 16 of 688, date edit 12 of 3104, hint 15 of 158, image combo box 6 of 266, progress bar 20 of 1738, tree list 141 of 3149 (at most 3 levels), vertical grid 8 of 2822, the others 0 or 1.
Icon edges rule, decided: symbolic icon rectangles (check, chevron, toolbar icons, folder, spin arrows) may be set aside as artwork when the rectangles are listed; borders, rings and text never. Button edit: flat 0, icon rectangles (186,10,6x9, 37 levels) and (177,13,8x10, 11 levels), nothing else above 7: matched. Toolbar button: flat 0 in 14 crops, nine icon rectangles in the toolbar crop plus (16,10,6x5) and (10,16,5x6) in each icon button, nothing outside them above 6 levels (focus ring corners, 1 to 2 px): matched. Popup menu: flat 0; the check mark (18x16, 66 px, 172 levels) and chevron (13x18, 74 px, 54 levels) are listed icon rectangles; the disabled row's label is 10 levels too dark on 133 px (glyph pixels, counted and reported): matched with that gap.

(2) Neper dark. The hint pair InverseOnSurface on InverseSurface is 10.2:1 and the face 14.4:1 against the page (the round 26 note says 12:1: overstated, passes). Pixel check (own method: 11 px median background, ink blobs merged along the row, text 4.5:1, other marks 3:1, disabled 38 % and 12 % mixes exempt) over all 268 sheets: text under 4.5:1 only in image_combo_box (the disabled field, 2.5:1) and native_dark2 (a Windows tooltip, 1.7:1). No failure in hint, hint_toolbar_*, hint_grid_header_name (text 10.2 and 9.6:1), tree_list, data_grid, group_box (frames 5.05 and 3.4:1), dialog (edge 4.1), popup_menu (3.9), menu_bar-open (3.4 to 3.9). The menu_bar_open_* sheets show only the bar (Windows-only pictures, empty under Neper), nothing light. Caveats that change no status: 22 sheets draw a one pixel border half on each of two pixels (2.3:1 each, one full pixel of Outline in total), tree list check boxes among them; 56 of 268 Neper dark sheets still carry neutral Windows greys (combo_box_open_*_head faces, image_combo_box_open_* lists, combo_box_flat_card, splitter_dialog white bars, image_combo_box_cell, scroll thumbs in data_grid_*, native_dark*), none is the screenshot of a status entry (31 sheets are referenced).

Spec-derived references (app bar, snackbar, tooltip, navigation bar, search bar, badge, date picker) are review or started, none matched.

# Looks audit 13, 2026-10-18 (rounds 23 and 24: Linux, Material, Cupertino, Neper dark)

Independent recompute at master 28622baff with new scratch comparers (not committed; none of the scripts the brief named). Renders in a clean worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe` copied in; no process was killed.

Result. Linux light and dark: matched 27, review 3, planned 16 each (96 %; the workers claimed 25 matched, I upgrade group box and vertical grid). Material light matched 2, review 32 (was 5 matched); Material dark matched 3, review 32 (was 6). Neper dark ready 27, review 3 (was 28 and 2). Cupertino unchanged (light review 28, started 11; dark review 36, started 3; no matched). Windows unchanged (light 19 matched, dark 10). Overall 75.9 %: Windows 76, Linux 96, Neper 98, macOS and iOS 52, Android 57. Downgrades: Material checkbox, radio, top app bar (both schemes), Neper dark hint. Upgrades: Linux group box and vertical grid (both schemes). Overlay `status.d/zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz-audit13.json`.

(1) Linux focus ring dispute: LF is right. With `scripts/linux/gallery.py`'s own helpers (WSL, Xvfb) I set FOCUSED and FOCUS_VISIBLE on a fresh DropDown and captured the same crop at 0, 100, 180, 400 and 840 ms of wall clock. Left edge, light: 0 ms no ring; 100 ms (223,226,230) faint; 180 ms (132,173,223) then (152,185,225), which is exactly the old committed reference (0 px differ from the pre-d0c54b3af file in both schemes, so audit 12's blended pixel is the 180 ms frame); 400 and 840 ms (129,171,223) twice, light, and (88,115,146) twice, dark. The 400 and 840 ms captures equal the committed settled `dropdown-focus@1x.png` byte for byte in both schemes (0 px differ), so the reference was not tuned to our output: the commit changed four reference files and one line of the gallery (settle 3 ticks of 60 ms to 20 for a focused flag). All other committed GTK focus references (button, entry, spin, mask, check, radio, combo entry) show the same two full pixels. Ours matches: dropdown-focus strict 8 px (ring corners and arrow), flat 0.

Linux sheets, own comparer (178 reliable light crops, every crop template-searched in the committed 1x sheet, reference on the window colour; same skips as audit 12): flat px off 0 in every control and both schemes except dialog (24 and 25 flat, 2241 and 2266 strict, 1109 of 1423 ink px differ: heading emboldening, review stays). Returned to matched, confirmed: combo box (strict 36 and 41, arrows and ring corners), text edit (49 and 58, search magnifier, eye, ring corners), mask edit (6 and 8), page control (0 and 1). Improved, confirmed: memo 0 strict, splitter 0, status bar 0, tree list (strict 282, 141 of 3149 ink px; was 3167 of 3295), data grid (32, 0 ink px), date edit (12), button edit (5 to 9: the folder icon, up to 37 levels, review stays), group box (0 to 1, upgrade), vertical grid (13, upgrade), toolbar button (80, Neper's own icon strokes, review stays). Glyph exactness by reference ink pixels differing by more than 2 levels: 0 in label, radio, radio group, list box, menu bar, check list box, memo, group box, splitter, status bar, data grid, spin edit, text edit, mask edit; 1 of 603 token edit; 2 to 28 of 3100 button, checkbox, date edit, page control; 141 of 3149 tree list; 640 of 6090 popup menu (chevron and check, symbolic icons); 1353 of 1668 dialog. The pseudo-language fix is real: the glyph drift of audit 12 is gone outside the dialog and popup menu.

(2) Material, own comparer (cell geometry from the sheet table, best placement within 3 px, every pixel of the cell, text and icon ink by the -notext twin). Flat metric as claimed: button 6 px off (text variants), icon button 49, checkbox 0, radio 0, switch 0, app bar 47, so the claims hold on structural pixels. But a 2 px outline is never flat, so strict counting shows: unchecked checkbox and radio in hover, focus and pressed draw on-surface-variant where Material Web (checkbox-styles.css hover-outline-color) and the M3 spec use on-surface: 116 to 124 px and 62 to 81 px per state, up to 47 levels, both schemes: checkbox and radio to review. Top app bar: Material Web has no app bar, the reference is plain CSS written from the spec by the same author (gallery.html says so, as for snackbar, tooltip, navigation bar, search bar, badge, date picker): not a capture, so not matched. Button outlined focus: ours draws the primary outline, the reference the resting grey (245 to 281 px, 76 levels); Material Web 2.2 has no focus outline token (it uses a focus ring), the capture turns the ring off, the M3 token set has primary: a reference and spec ambiguity, left as claimed but noted. Reference choices: baseline palette (#FEF7FF surface, #6750A4 primary) is the M3 baseline; Roboto from the Windows fonts; hover 8 %, pressed 10 % (Material Web ships 12 %, the spec 10 %), focus 10 % forced through the ripple's hover opacity, not a real :focus; tracking set to 0 (deviation: Neper has no letter spacing). All defensible. `matshade.e`: analytic (erfc blur of a signed distance field), 150 lines, no baked data; the layer constants are fitted numbers. `looks_scale_check.py` does not handle Material (0 of 0 sheets). Toolbar button dark (the icon-button cells of the fab sheet): flat 2 px, outlined focus as above; stays matched.

(3) Cupertino: confirmed spec-derived only; statuses are review or started, no matched anywhere. `cupertino_spec_check.py` is not circular for sizes and colours (hard-coded Apple numbers measured from rendered sheets), but it checks the source tokens against constants written by the same author, its state checks only demand more than 20 differing px from rest, and some ranges are loose (tinted 12 to 30 %, disabled 12 to 40 %). Metrics checked against iOS 17 and macOS 14: system colours with their dark pairs, label alphas (0.6, 0.3), separator (0.29, 0.6), fills (0.2, 0.16, 0.12; 0.36, 0.32, 0.24), switch 51 x 31 with 27 thumb, slider 4 and 28, segmented 32, stepper 94 x 32, search field 36 and radius 10, activity indicators 20 and 37, alert 270 and radius 14, navigation bar 44, tab bar 49, row 44, macOS push button 22 (min 64), checkbox and radio 14: right. Doubtful: the "large" button is asserted 44 pt; UIKit's large configuration is about 50 pt (44 is the minimum tap target); the Dark page is #1C1C1E, which is the elevated grouped level (the base is #000000), declared as such.

(4) Neper dark. Tokens, independent: Text on page 14.4:1, muted 10.9, primary 10.9, error 10.9, Outline 5.84 (4.5 on container high, 3.87 on surface variant), on primary 7.7; OutlineVariant 1.98 is decoration only. Focus rings: 96 ring-like primary components over the 268 sheets, contrast against the colour outside the ring at least 7.1:1 (inside a primary fill 1.18 but the outside carries it). Text over 4562 components: 125 under 4.5, all disabled 38 % ink (2.5 to 3.9) except light Windows hint chrome: five `hint_toolbar_*` sheets (Text on #FFFFE1, 1.27:1) and `hint_grid_header_name` (1.73:1), enabled hint text: hint ready to review. Other Windows-only sheets leak light chrome into the dark directory (combo flat card, image combo cell, splitter dialog, page control bottom, native_dark*): no text failure measured, not dark mapped. Neper light: `git diff aca4d1f88^1 28622baff -- docs/ui/looks/neper` is empty.

(5) Re-renders at 28622baff, byte for byte equal to the committed PNGs, 1x and 1.5x, light and dark: Linux text_edit, radio, popup_menu, combo_box, hint, date_edit, dialog, memo; Material radio, dialog, tree_list, scroll_bar; Cupertino memo, list, tooltip; Windows popup_menu, syslink, checkbox_dialog, label, list_box, radio_dialog, group_box_dialog, data_grid_dialog_states. Scale check, one sheet at a time, all pass (exact / within a pixel at 1.25, 1.5, 2.0): linux:tree_list 99.3 / 99.5, 99.7, 99.7; linux:combo_box 98.3 / 100, 100, 99.4 / 100; linux:page_control 99.4 / 99.8, 99.8 / 100, 100 (dark 98.2 to 100).

Largest remaining gaps: Android (32 review per scheme, five references are spec-written CSS), macOS and iOS (no capture, 52 %), Windows dark (16 review), Linux dialog (heading emboldening, 1353 of 1668 ink px), popup menu icons, Neper dark leaked light Windows chrome (hint, data grid, tree list).

# Looks audit 12, 2026-10-18 (round 22 merge: Linux light and dark, Windows, Neper dark)

Independent recompute at master aca4d1f88 (merge of looks22-merge, previous first parent 0ff70c81f) with new scratch comparers (not committed; none of `scripts/linux_cmp_*.py`, `linux_flat_la.py`, `looks_*.py`, `neper_dark_contrast.py`). Linux metric as audits 10 and 11: all 186 crops per scheme of the three manifests (`ref/linux/{light,dark}`, composited on the window colour) searched in the committed 1x Linux sheets, best placement within 3 px; reference flat pixel = 8 neighbours within 2 levels; off = ours differs by more than 2 levels; glyph pixels within 2 px of text ink reported excluded and counted. Added in this audit: the strict count (every pixel outside text ink, so thin rings, icons and lines, which a flat-only metric cannot see) and, for text, the share of reference ink pixels that differ by more than 2 levels. Unreliable crops left out: spin-hover and spin-active (forced flags), dropdown-open and popover-menu-open (the sheet draws the button above; first 22 rows ignored), tooltip (CSS approximation), spin-focus and spin-focus-ring (value 0, no cell: the sheet's focus cell shows 50).

Result. Windows light matched 19, review 10, started 1 (84 %); Windows dark matched 10, review 16, started 4 (68 %): no change. Linux light and Linux dark: matched 21, review 9 each (88 %; the workers claimed 25 and 5). Neper dark: ready 21, review 9 (88 %; was 22 and 8). Neper light 100 %. Downgrades: combo box, text edit, mask edit, page control (Linux, both schemes) and group box (Neper dark). Upgrades: none. Overlay `status.d/zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz-audit12.json`.

(1) Linux. Claimed matched and confirmed in both schemes (0 flat px off, glyphs excluded and counted, in every reliable crop): button (31 crops; strict 32 px, at most 12 per crop), check list box, checkbox (strict 37, the switch focus rings), data grid, hint, image combo box, label, list box, menu bar, popup menu, progress bar, radio, radio group, scroll bar, spin edit, splitter, status bar, token edit, tree list, memo. Downgraded: combo box (the focus ring: GTK's second ring pixel is blended, 153,186,225 against the outer 132,173,223; ours draws both at 129,172,223, 24 levels off along the whole ring, 766 px in dropdown-focus and 823 over the control; the flat metric cannot see a band, but a focus ring is always compared); text edit (entry-warning 23 flat px glyphs excluded, 28 counted, both schemes); mask edit (entry-mask 22, entry-mask-focus 20, entry-mask-disabled 24 flat px, counted 108 light and 84 dark); page control (adw-tabbar 47 excluded, 101 counted; adw-viewswitcher 3). In the text edit, mask edit and page control crops every differing pixel is a glyph pixel (checked on the diff images), but the grey, brown and placeholder text is under the 60-level ink threshold of audit 10, so it is not set apart. Audit 10 held the same crops at review (21, 34 and 64 px); the claims would pass only with a better ink detector, which is why they go back. Review stays: button edit (0 flat, 0 strict), toolbar button (strict 6), vertical grid, group box, dialog (alert-dialog 5 excluded, 32 counted).

CFF hinter for Cantarell, glyph shape (share of reference ink pixels more than 2 levels off, light / dark): exact in label 0 of 800 / 0 of 826, radio 0 of 2585 / 0 of 2304, radio group 0 / 0, list box 0 of 1942 / 0 of 1940, menu bar 0 of 672, check list box 0 of 324, spin edit 0 of 641, memo 0 of 1184 (at its own placement), token edit 1 of 595, checkbox 5 of 4201 / 0 of 4479, image combo box 8 of 927, hint 27 of 445 / 0, progress bar 11 of 1080 / 16 of 2124. So the port is correct where the text goes through it. It is not exact elsewhere: text edit 1330 of 2674 (the first one or two glyphs are right, then the advances drift up to a pixel: "me text" of "Some text" differs), mask edit 559 of 908, date edit 2479 of 2871, data grid 1284 of 1640, tree list 3167 of 3295, dialog 1343 of 1657, page control 2877 of 3572, popup menu 664 of 5979, status bar 187 of 753, splitter 372 of 518, group box 283 of 531, button edit 111 of 258, toolbar button 149 of 2177, button 44 of 5660, combo box 79 of 1568, vertical grid 1083 of 2432. Flat-pixel numbers hide this because glyph pixels are rarely flat: it is the largest Linux gap. Popup menu (strict 176 px, up to 54 levels): the submenu chevron is bolder than GTK's and the check mark is off, both symbolic icons.

References. Identical crops, so the state is not evidence: entry hover, active and focus equal entry normal; dropdown-open-button equals dropdown-normal; textview-focus equals textview; progress-0 equals levelbar; scale-hover equals scale-active; scrollbar hover equals normal in both orientations; paned equals paned-wide. Every other state differs from rest by real pixels, light and dark differ. The 114 reference files changed by the merge are the lb re-captures (840 ms settle) and add no new rest-equal state; the la and lc additions are sheets, not reference crops.

(2) Windows. The merge changed 96 light sheets (popup menu, page control, toolbar button, tree list expanded, native dark) and 3 dark ones; no other committed Windows PNG changed, and 6 random other Windows sheets re-rendered byte for byte (below), so nothing that matched regressed. Own comparer (reference sheet cut into its pieces, template search), flat px off glyphs counted, before (0ff70c81f) to after: toolbar button 133 to 0 (the strip's differing px 5249 to 7), toolbar button row 145 to 0 (7604 to 38), toolbar button hot 150 to 0 in each of 9 crops (4934 to 38): matched confirmed. No chrome is stamped: in a scratch worktree with the tile path of `windows_glyph_toggle_art` switched off the render differs from the committed one only in 25 (row) and 17 (sheet) components, none larger than 20 x 21 px, the 16 x 17 tiles; buttons, grips, separators and hover boxes are drawn. Popup menu before to after: popup_menu_file 238 to 0, popup_menu_cards 225 to 0, popup_menu_sel_cards 184 to 0, popup_menu_help 31 to 0, popup_menu_search 266 to 33 (13 glyphs excluded), popup_menu_dialog 294 to 14, popup_menu_sel_view_1 109 to 13; a real improvement (v3's own 16 x 16 pictures), stays review as claimed. Page control stats 82 to 66 (61 to 69 per crop) and tree list expanded 1656 to 1630: review confirmed. Windows dark, native 1x captures, glyphs counted, before to after: edit-multiline-explorer 0 flat (1203 to 187 differing px, glyph rows), tooltip-rest 1738 to 177, tooltip-balloon 1797 to 236 (the workers' 122 and 165 use another placement), spin-pressed-up 21 to 12, spin-pressed-down 22 to 9, rest 7, focus 23: the review claims hold, no upgrade.

(3) Neper dark (no reference screen: never matched). Tokens (`fill_dark`): on surface #E1E2E9 on the page #121318 14.4:1, on surface variant 10.9, primary 10.9, error 10.9, on primary label 7.7, outline #8D909E 5.84, outline variant #434653 1.98. Sheet computation over all 268 1.0 sheets: 3690 text components (7 to 22 px tall, at least 1.5 times as wide), core pixel against the local background; 61 sit at 3.0 to 4.5 and 24 below 3.0. All of them are disabled content (38 % ink, #616267 on the page 3.05, on a field 2.8 to 2.96), selection fills or hairlines picked up as text; no enabled text is under 4.5:1. Boundaries: the edges of field, combo, memo, date, mask, button edit, token edit and splitter use outline (5.84:1); checkbox box 7.6:1; list box and check list box 3.93:1; scroll thumb 4.22:1; focus rings are primary, 10.9:1 on the page (not checked against filled faces). Failure: the group box frame (#3F414E, 1.8:1) and its row hairline (#434653, 1.98:1) are under 3:1 and the frame is all that identifies the group: group box ready to review. Data grid and tree list use the 1.98:1 hairline too (already review). Neper light: `git diff 0ff70c81f aca4d1f88 -- docs/ui/looks/neper` is empty; the re-rendered Neper sheets equal the committed ones except neper/data_grid_bottom@1.5x (audit 11 saw the same kind of drift on data_grid_main@1.5x at both commits).

(4) Baked data. Linux, all small tiles: `linux_card.e` 12 x 12 corners (144 px each), 20 x 20 check box states (400), 46 x 28 switch (1288), a 48 value glow; `linuxmark_lb.e` 14 x 14 alpha masks (196 bytes, five) of libadwaita's check and radio; `linuxspin_lb.e` the spinner's 32 x 32 8-bit raster (1024) from the Adwaita SVG, not a capture; `linuxsoft_lb.e` and `linuxshadow_l2.e` fitted constants only; `adwicon_l2.e` SVG source. No source reads `ref/` except the Cantarell font files and the sheet harness's `-vs-ref` strips. Windows: `wintoolicon.e` 27 tiles of 16 x 17 (7344 px), `winmenuicon.e` 122 tiles of 16 x 16 (31,232 px), `winpageicon.e` 11 tiles of 16 x 16, `wintreeicon.e` 15 tiles of 22 x 16 (5280 px): v3 artwork cut from the captures (alpha 0 = the face, the tiles keep the pale halo), none larger than 22 x 16, none chrome. No large baked region and no new stamp in round 22. The data grid stamps (`docs/ui/looks/marks/*.txt`, 5 files, 188 KB; 51,796 px over 34 Windows light sheets in audit 9) are unchanged and still do not count; the data grid stays review.

(5) Render and scale. Re-rendered in a clean worktree at aca4d1f88 with the committed `neper-self.exe` copied in: 10 random Linux apps (linux_tree_list, linux_date_edit, linux_radio, linux_memo, linux_toolbar_button, linux_hint, linux_checkbox_switch, linux_image_combo_box, linux_button, linux_status_bar), 6 random Windows apps (title_bar, memo, status_bar, group_box_dialog, button, data_grid_bottom) and toolbar_button: every light and dark PNG, 1x and 1.5x, and the vs-ref strips are byte for byte the committed ones; one Neper file, data_grid_bottom@1.5x, differs. `looks_scale_check.py`, one sheet at a time, exact / within a device pixel at 1.25, 1.5 and 2.0, all pass: linux:tree_list 99.3 / 99.5, 99.7 / 99.7, 99.7 / 99.7 (dark 99.2 to 99.7); linux:page_control 99.5 / 99.8, 99.8 / 100, 100 (dark 98.2 to 100); linux:combo_box 98.3 / 100, 100, 99.4 / 100; linux:status_bar 98.6 / 100, 100, 100; toolbar_button 98.4 / 100, 100, 98.4 / 100; popup_menu_dialog 100 at all three; tree_list_expanded 100 at all three; page_control_stats_1 99.4, 98.8, 92.5 exact (100 within a pixel). `linux:text_edit` and `linux:label` run 0 of 0 sheets in the script, so they were replaced.

Largest remaining gaps. Linux: glyph drift outside the label family (text edit, mask edit, date edit, data grid, tree list, dialog, page control, popup menu), the combo box focus ring blend, the popup menu chevron and tick. Windows: popup menu search and view menus (33 and 13 px), page control stats (61 to 69), tree list (1630 px all-pixel, mostly text), the data grid (stamps hide about 18,000 px), dark tooltips (177, 236). Neper dark: group frame contrast; toolbar button, menu bar, popup menu, text edit, image combo box, tree list, data grid and dialog stay review.

# Looks audit 11, 2026-10-18 (Linux light and dark: data grid, dialog, combo box after the drawn shadows)

Independent recompute at master 3df9dff35 (merge of looks21-shadow) with a new comparer (scratch, not `scripts/linux_cmp_*.py`, not committed). Metric as audit 10: reference crops of `ref/linux/{light,dark}` composited on the window colour and placed in the committed 1x Linux sheets (the combo box cells at the sheet's cells, the best placement within 3 px; the popover crop at its popover); reference flat pixel = 8 neighbours within 2 levels; off = ours differs by more than 2 levels in any channel; excluded only glyph pixels within 2 px of text ink and coloured artwork (symbolic arrows and ticks are not text and count); glyph-counted numbers are given too. Borders, fills, shadows and focus rings always compared. The first 22 rows of the dropdown-open sheet crop (where the sheet draws the button above the popover, no reference) are ignored.

Result. Data grid and dialog matched: confirmed, both schemes. Combo box review: confirmed, both schemes (not upgraded: the drop-down arrow). No status changes; Linux stays at 77 % on the page (Light 77, Dark 77; matched 13, review 17 per scheme). Overlay `status.d/zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz-audit11.json` only corrects the combo box notes and gaps.

Flat px off, glyphs excluded / glyphs counted (Light and Dark equal unless noted). Data grid columnview 0 / 0; steep px off 36 Light, 34 Dark (all within 8 levels but 2 and 4 px; 2774 / 2807 steep px lie in text). Dialog alert-dialog 0 / 52 (the 52 are text edges: body text 4 px wider, as the gap says); steep 152 Light, 918 Dark outside glyphs, of which 89 are over 8 levels (button label pieces not recognised as text) and the rest 2 to 8 levels in the card face and shadow. Focus-ring states (border band, steep): dropdown-focus-ring 39 Light, 48 Dark (max 10 levels, ring corners); combo-entry-focus 27 + 26 inside = 53 Light, 58 Dark; dark active 17 (3 levels). The first manifest's dropdown-focus (a ring of 1 px plus a fractional px) is a different capture than the l2 focus-ring ours follows: 770 Light and 405 Dark steep px off, up to 31 levels; the claim used the focus-ring crop. Date edit open popover: no GTK reference of the date edit's popover; against dropdown-open's shadow at the same distance from the body (left, right, above, diagonal) ours is within 2 levels in both schemes; below the body the profile equals the reference's shifted by one row (the reference body's edge row is fractional), so the shape is the same.

(a) Baked data. `linuxshadow_l2.e` has none (three fitted Gaussian layers per shadow, 3 numbers each; `n > 640` px wide draws nothing); `adwicon_l2.e` is SVG source only; `linux_card.e` bakes only small tiles: four 12 x 12 corners (576 px), the 20 x 20 check box, four states (1600 px), the 46 x 28 switch, two schemes (2576 px) and a 48 value top glow. Per control: data grid none (the scroll glow is a cone and an ellipse of 12 fitted constants per scheme, `scroll_level`, thumb fixed to the track), dialog none (card face, rim and shadow drawn by `card_node`; card height 142 fixed by the caller), combo box none (arrow and tick are SVG).
