# Looks audit 10, 2026-10-18 (Linux light and Linux dark, round 20; Windows regression)

Independent recompute at master 81e68f2bd with a new comparer (scratch, not `scripts/linux_cmp_l*.py`, not committed). Every crop of the three manifests (186 per scheme, `ref/linux/{light,dark}`, composited on the window colour) is located by template search in the committed 1x Linux sheets (the control's own sheets first, then all; the best placement within 3 px by fewest off pixels; `-vs-ref` and `composite` files never searched). Reference flat pixel = all 8 neighbours within 2 levels; off = ours differs by more than 2 levels in any channel. Excluded only: glyph pixels within 2 px of text ink (ink = more than 60 levels from the median of a 25 x 13 window, runs of 13 or more not ink, a piece is text when a neighbour is within 6 px or it is 12 px or wider and at most 16 high) and coloured artwork (saturated non-flat components up to 30 x 30, grown by 2 px). Isolated glyphs (a lone digit, a calendar number) are not recognised as text, so the counts are a little strict; symbolic icons, triangles and ticks are always counted. Tolerance for "matched": at most 5 flat px off in every reliable crop, both schemes.

Result. Workers claimed 14 matched, 16 review, 0 started in each scheme (the brief said 19; the merged overlays say 14). After this audit Linux light and Linux dark: matched 11, review 19, started 0 each. Completion: Linux 75 % (was 79 %). Downgrades (matched to review): data grid (baked scroll glow), image combo box (6 px), status bar (header bar icons, 41 and 14 px), page control (64, 105, 11, 4 px), tree list (21 px, disclosure triangles). Upgrades (review to matched): radio and radio group (0 flat px in all reliable crops). Overlay `status.d/zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz-audit10.json`.

Flat px off, glyphs excluded (reliable crops, sum; light and dark equal unless noted). Confirmed matched: button edit 4, memo 1, token edit 0, check list box 0, list box 0, hint 0, menu bar 0, splitter 0, scroll bar 0 (8 crops). Review stays: label 0 (all text), progress bar 0 in 11 crops (scale-marks has no cell), radio 0, radio group 0, button 46 (button-linked, a 5 px column at x 124), checkbox 76 (switch-on-normal 45 and switch-off-disabled 31: the knob shadow), combo box 225 (the arrow icon, 17 to 33 px at 184 levels, in every state), date edit 126 (calendar numbers, mostly unrecognised glyphs; the 22 px right edge of date-button), mask edit 21, popup menu 7, text edit 34 (search-entry icons), toolbar button 184 (the flat icon crops 75, 22, 17), spin edit (spin50-* 5 px each; spin-vertical 127; the manifest's spin-normal and spin-disabled 28 are a lone digit), group box 14, dialog 21, vertical grid 1.

References. Identical crops (so the state is not evidence): entry hover, active and focus equal entry rest (0 px differ), textview-focus equals textview, scrollbar hover equals normal for both orientations, dropdown-open-button equals dropdown-normal; scale-hover equals scale-active; paned equals paned-wide (stated). The first manifest's spin-hover and spin-active (forced flags on every child) match nothing in ours (1928 and 1046 px) and are replaced by spin50-*; popover-menu-open and dropdown-open are popover surfaces on their own, ours draws the button above (3188 px in the top 22 rows of dropdown-open, 6943 for popover-menu-open: no cell, ignored); tooltip is the .tooltip CSS node approximation (tooltip-real is the real one, 0 px). Credible: every other state differs from rest by real pixels, light and dark are distinct (only the suggested button, progress-100 and the always dark tooltip are equal across schemes). Hover and pressed are state flags on the library, not a real pointer, as the workers say.

Baked reference pixels (`linux_card.e`, `linuxshadow_l2.e`; `adwicon_l2.e` holds the Adwaita symbolic SVGs, source assets drawn by e.gfx.svg, not rendered pixels). In a scratch worktree the six painters (corners, pixels, glow, alert shadow, top glow, popover shadow) return at once; the changed sheets are check list box, combo box open, data grid, date edit open, dialog, list box, vertical grid. Flat px that match only because of the baked tiles (Light / Dark, summed over crops; all changed px in brackets): data grid 3356 / 3824 (the 53 x 198 scroll glow; 3830 / 4312), dialog 17,348 / 60 (the alert shadow; 21,191 / 1053), combo box 2352 / 522 (popover shadow; 5065 / 1071), list box 6 / 0 (card corners; 309 / 132), check list box 2 / 0 (corners and the 20 x 20 check box; 123 / 60), vertical grid 4 / 0 (switch, corners, top glow; 518 / 107). Without them data grid, dialog and combo box fail by thousands of px. Recommendation: the small tiles (12 x 12 corners, the 20 x 20 check box, the 46 x 28 switch, the SVG icons) may count; the scroll glow, the alert shadow and the popover shadow should not, so data grid is downgraded here, and dialog and combo box stay review. A drawn shadow (blur or stacked discs) would be the proper fix.

Render and scale checks. All 100 Linux sheets (37 apps, light, dark, 1.5x and the -vs-ref strips, with the popup menu hover and active runs) rendered from a clean master worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe` are byte for byte the committed PNGs (git shows no change). Windows regression: ten apps picked at random (cup_button_edit, cup_text_field, material_switch, material_vertical_grid, button_arrows, edits_hover, status_bar, title_bar, memo, data_grid_dialog_states) rendered at master and at c96af6476 give 73 non-Linux PNGs, all byte for byte equal, and equal to the committed ones; `git diff c96af6476 master` over windows, windows-dark, neper, neper-dark, cupertino, cupertino-dark, material and material-dark changes 27 files, all Windows check list box and popup menu sheets from the Windows worker (none from the Linux work, nothing in the other seven looks). `looks_scale_check.py` is Windows only (it forces NEPER_LOOKS_ONLY=windows, reads the windows folder, and run_linux ignored NEPER_LOOKS_SCALE and NOTEXT), so in a scratch worktree run_linux gained the same two environment variables, the script reads `linux/`, and four sheets were added to the table, one at a time at 1.25 / 1.5 / 2.0 exact column: progress_bar 99.9 / 100 / 100, list_box 100 / 100 / 100, scroll_bar 99.0 / 100 / 100, tree_list 99.3 / 99.7 / 99.7 (all pass). Those edits are not committed; the committed script cannot check Linux sheets.

Largest gaps (flat px, one crop). Light: spin-vertical 127, page control adw-viewswitcher 105, calendar 104, toolbar flat-icon-hover 75, adw-tabbar 64, button-linked 46, switch-on-normal 45, status bar headerbar-subtitle 41, combo box arrow 26 to 62, tree list triangles 21; Dark are the same except adw-viewswitcher 3. Unreliable states: the three entry states, textview focus, scrollbar hover.

# Looks audit 9, 2026-10-18 (Windows light and Windows dark, hinted ClearType text, stamped marks)

Independent recompute at master 41dae2572 (hinted ClearType text merged at a0b25ae4b), with a new comparer (scratch, not `scripts/looks_*.py`; not committed). Metric as audit 8: reference flat pixel = pixel of an equal-colour run of 11 or more whose 8 neighbours are in runs and whose colour occurs 30 or more times in the crop (light; dark: the runs grown by one pixel, no colour count); off = ours differs by more than 2 levels in any channel. Light: each `ref/windows` sheet is cut by an XY cut (gap 6) against its border colour and each crop is compared in place at offsets of 3 px, then by a masked template search of the whole sheet (ours is often the reference plus a margin or caption). Dark: every 1x native dark capture (219 control and popup rectangles, plus 3 px) is searched, nothing cut from ours, in the sheets of its control and the native_dark, syslink and title_bar sheets (best 6 placements per sheet). Only excluded: v3 coloured artwork (components of coloured non-flat reference pixels up to 30 x 30, grown by 2 px; the boxes are found by the script, not listed by hand). NEW: glyph pixels within 2 px of text ink (ink = more than 60 levels from the commonest colour of a 25 x 13 window, runs of 13 or more are not ink, a piece is text when a neighbour is within 6 px or it is a word 12 px or wider and at most 16 high) are reported both ways: "glyphs excluded" as in audit 8, and "glyphs counted". Dialog sheets (whole dialogs, no per-control reference) are left out. The crop is the bounding box of the XY piece with no margin (a 3 px margin pulls in the neighbouring tile's edge and fails radio_states by 50 px per crop).

Result. Windows (light): matched 17, review 12, started 1 (workers claimed 20 / 9 / 1). Windows dark: matched 10, review 16, started 4 (claimed 11 / 15 / 4). Completion: Windows Light 81 %, Dark 68 %, the Windows flavor 75 %, all five flavors 51 % (audit 8: 85, 69, 77, 51). Upgrade: scroll bar light (review to matched). Downgrades: check list box, page control, popup menu and toolbar button light (matched to review), label dark (matched to review). The overlay is `status.d/zzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzzz-audit9.json`.

Windows light, 270 sheets, 587 crops: glyphs excluded 6663 flat px off in all (426 crops at 0), glyphs counted 11,040 (380 crops at 0). Per control (crops, crops above 5 px with glyphs counted, px excluded / counted): label 12, 0, 0 / 0; check box 29, 0, 0 / 0; radio 20 and radio group 17, 0, 0 / 0; edits (spin, mask, button edit, text edit) 37, 0, 0 / 3; token edit 3, 0, 0 / 0; image combo box 29, 0, 0 / 0; group box 4, 0, 0 / 0; menu bar 21, 0, 0 / 0; splitter 8 and status bar 8, 0, 0 / 0; hint 6, 1, 0 / 8; list box 3, 0, 0 / 3; button 84, 1, 18 / 19 (button_kinds, the arrow bitmap); scroll bar 18, 0, 0 / 0; combo box 44, 4, 39 / 68 (review, max 19). Matched claims that fail: check list box 15 crops, 12 above 5 px, 0 / 301 (check_list_box_dialog 87: a 1 px orange column of ours at x 32 that the capture lacks); page control 21, 13 above 5, 1 / 757 (single tab-top pixels, orange in ours); popup menu 77, 55 above 5, 4079 / 5561 (the bold group headers sit 1 px high in ours and item text drifts in the same rows; worst 296); toolbar button 11, 11 above 20, 418 / 432 (one bar row at y 36, 231 to 233 in the capture and 240 in ours; audits 7 and 8 counted such rows as artwork, this audit does not because they are not coloured). Review claims unchanged: data grid 67 crops, none at 0, 1922 / 2766 (worst data_grid_main 168); tree list 11, none at 0, 102 / 858 (tree_list_expanded 133); vertical grid 11, none at 0, 84 / 258; text edit 34 of 37 at 0 but no TcxTextEdit capture. Memo, progress bar, date edit and dialog have no reference sheet: unchanged. Text: with glyphs counted the matched controls with real text (label, check box, radio, image combo box, menu bar, status bar) are at 0, so hinted ClearType does match v3 on those sheets; the residue is in popup menu headers, grid cells and tree text.

Windows dark, native 1x captures (px glyphs excluded / glyphs counted, summed over captures; worst capture). Matched claims: button 0 / 191 (22); check box 0 / 381 (28); scroll bar 36 / 36 (4; 5 of 14 at 0); label 0 / 523 (label-disabled 240, syslink 63 to 67), downgraded because a label is only text; mask edit follows the single-line edit captures; the seven derived ones (toolbar button, menu bar, status bar, group box, page control, splitter, progress bar) have no dark capture of the control (native stays light for most), unchanged. Review: combo box 9 / 299 (49); image combo box shares them; radio 194 / 254 (28); spin edit 70 / 70 (21); popup menu 25 / 584 (95); text edit and memo 2425 / 3837 (vscroll 1583, explorer 1137); hint 1312 / 2404; the rest have no dark capture of the control (grid, tree, vertical grid, date edit) and stay as audit 8 left them. No dark claim is upgraded. Light native captures were not compared.

Stamped marks. The marks are read only by the data grid sheet apps (`examples/looks/data_grid*.e`: `Look.ring_marks` text, inline in the apps or loaded from `docs/ui/looks/marks/*.txt`, joined with `bar_marks` and drawn by `ring_marks_over` in `lib/e/ui/datagrid.e`, Windows light only). In a scratch worktree with `ring_marks_over` made to return at once and the five apps rendered whole, 34 Windows sheets change (51,796 pixels); no other sheet changes (tree list, vertical grid, scroll bar and every other control use no marks). Per sheet: stamped px / of them ref-flat and now equal / glyphs-excluded flat off with stamps > without: bottom 2779/567/251>800; bottom2 2904/861/238>1054; bottom3 1165/298/119>387; dialogs_sort 131/66/22>84; group 4747/1631/59>1675; group2 3553/1681/75>1722; group3 2202/891/47>926; hover 1460/400/14>374; hover2 1416/387/6>353; hover3 1294/233/6>237; hover4 1214/164/24>168; hover5 1305/184/6>161; hover6 1214/166/6>172; hover7 1166/166/6>172; hover8 2454/914/34>926; main 2656/842/141>937; missing_hover 634/272/17>272; missing_states 1270/544/34>544; shortcuts_hover 1518/1147/92>1229; shortcuts_hover2 1012/578/76>654; shortcuts_hover3 1012/578/76>654; shortcuts_hover4 419/272/54>326; shortcuts_states 1418/855/84>938; shortcuts_states2 1038/604/76>678; shortcuts_states3 468/288/54>341; sort_hover 1140/879/110>934; sort_states 981/601/107>663; states 541/16/7>23; states_pressed 635/161/0>144; top 1747/353/10>346; top2 1588/196/10>206; top3 1588/196/10>206; top4 1588/196/10>206; top5 1539/222/41>263. Totals: 51,796 stamped px, 17,409 of the matched flat px come from stamps, glyphs excluded 1922 with stamps against 18,775 without (glyphs counted 2766 against 19,769). Recommendation: stamps do not count. A stamp copies the capture's pixel into our render, so a sheet that equals the capture proves nothing about the control; without them the data grid is 18,775 px off in 67 crops and stays review (the orchestrator's hold is right). Count a stamped pixel only after the control draws it, and keep the marks files as the worklist of what the grid still draws wrong (column line ends, header icon column, corner, thumb ends, chip glyphs, frame colours).

Render and scale checks. Eight random Windows sheets (image_combo_box_cell, hint_toolbar_cards_expand, data_grid_dialogs, page_control_bottom_states, combo_box_search_held, data_grid_hover3, data_grid_hover8, combo_box_open_color2_head) rendered from a clean master worktree with the committed `neper-self.exe` copied in: the light and dark PNGs are byte for byte the committed ones, 16 of 16 (scroll_bar_native, scroll_bar_main and the dark native sheet too). The Neper sheet combo_box_open_color2_head (and its 1.5x) differs from the commit, outside these looks. Grayscale switch: NEPER_TEXT_AA=grayscale reproduces the pre-ClearType text (blobs at 9d42c1c20^) with 0 differing pixels for label, button_states, popup_menu, popup_menu_file, group_box and status_bar (light) and the dark label, button_states, group_box and status_bar; the dark popup_menu sheets differ (6076 and 10,354 px) and were not traced; group_box_dialog was not re-rendered. `looks_scale_check.py` on 8 sheets, one at a time, exact column at 1.25 / 1.5 / 2.0 (all pass): popup_menu_dialog 100 / 100 / 100, page_control_stats 99.4 / 98.9 / 92.3, popup_menu_sel_file_1 100 / 100 / 100, hint_toolbar_cards_f 100 / 100 / 100, combo_box_open_color_head 100 / 100 / 100, data_grid 99.7 / 99.7 / 99.9, splitter_dialog 98.9 / 98.9 / 100, image_combo_box_open_art_head 100 / 100 / 100; `hint`, `data_grid_shortcuts_hover2` and `data_grid_shortcuts_states` are not run by the script (0 of 0) and were replaced.

Largest remaining gaps (flat px, one crop, glyphs counted). Light: popup_menu_sel_view_1 296, data_grid_main 168, data_grid_bottom 165, tree_list_expanded 133, check_list_box_dialog 87, page_control_stats 68, toolbar_button_row 57, vertical_grid_states 28; stamps still hide about 18,000 data grid px. Dark: edit-multiline-vscroll 1583, explorer 1137, label-disabled 240, popup submenu 95; no reference for tree list, data grid, vertical grid, date edit and token edit.

# Looks audit 8, 2026-10-17 (Windows light and Windows dark, claims changed since audit 7)

Independent recompute at master b3127e8a8 (round 17 and the diag 16 overlay merged), with a second new comparer (scratch, not `scripts/looks_*.py`, not audit 7's). Method as audit 7: reference flat pixel = pixel of an equal-colour run of 11 or more (light: colour 30 or more times in the crop and all 8 neighbours in runs; dark: the runs grown by one pixel); off = ours differs by more than 2 levels in any channel. Light: each reference sheet of `ref/windows` is cut by an XY cut (gap 6) against its border colour and every crop is compared in place (offsets of 3 px, then a template search of the whole sheet). Dark: every 1x native dark capture of the claimed controls is cut to each control and popup rectangle plus 3 px and searched, with nothing cut from ours, in all the `windows-dark` sheets (best 6 placements by squared difference, lowest count kept). Excluded, and only these: glyph pixels within 2 px of text ink (ink = more than 60 levels from the commonest colour of a 25 x 13 window; runs of 13 or more are not ink; a piece is text when a neighbour piece in its line is within 6 px, or when it is a fused word 12 px or wider and at most 16 high; isolated chevrons, ticks, dots, carets, selections and borders stay in). No v3 artwork rectangle was needed: the only artwork pixel outside the text rule is one coloured icon pixel group in tree_list (140,238, salmon against #EFEFEF), listed as a gap, not excluded from the numbers below. Numbers below are flat px off after the text rule unless "all" is said. For the two dark claims upgraded this round the excluded pixels were mapped out per capture: all remaining difference sits in the word glyphs (ClearType colour fringes against greyscale), none on a ring, corner, arrow, icon or fill.

Result. Windows (light): matched 20, review 9, started 1 (unchanged, workers and audit agree). Windows dark: matched 11, review 15, started 4; the matched set is the seven maintainer-accepted DERIVED ones (toolbar button, menu bar, status bar, group box, page control, splitter, progress bar: no reference exists, nothing to compare), button and label (confirmed here), and audit 7's carry-overs (check box, mask edit, scroll bar), which this round did not touch and this audit did not re-measure; the page counts 11. Downgrades: none. Upgrades beyond the workers' claims: none. Page: Windows Light 85 %, Dark 69 %, the Windows flavor 77 %, all five flavors 51 %.

Windows dark, claims changed since audit 7.
- Button (review to matched) CONFIRMED. All 18 native dark button captures (rest, hover, pressed, disabled, focus, default x4, hover-pressed, icon x3, split x5) at 0 flat px off outside the word glyphs, placed in native_dark.png and native_dark3.png. The split buttons' arrows and the icon (audit 7's button-split-disabled 9 px) are equal; the 106 to 136 px per capture that the text rule sets apart are the word "Button" only. Sheets native_dark3 and native_dark re-rendered from master equal the committed PNGs.
- Label (review to matched) CONFIRMED. label-rest and label-disabled 0 (native_dark.png), syslink rest, hover, disabled and focus 0 (syslink.png). The sheets re-rendered from master (syslink, native_dark) equal the committed ones. Unverified states stay as the diag overlay lists them.

# Looks audit 7, 2026-10-17 (Windows light and Windows dark only)

Independent recompute at HEAD 320d4f89a after round 15, with a new comparer written for this audit (scratch, not `scripts/looks_*.py`). Reference flat pixel = pixel of an equal-colour run of 11 or more (light: colour 30 or more times in the crop and all 8 neighbours flat; dark: the runs grown by one pixel, no colour count); off = ours differs by more than 2 levels in any channel. Light: the reference sheet is cut by an XY cut (gap 6) against its own border colour, each crop is compared in place (offsets of 3 px searched, then a masked template search of the whole sheet; a crop that only matches elsewhere is reported as moved). Dark: every native dark capture at 1x (197), cut to its control and popup rectangles plus 3 px, is searched in the native_dark sheets and the sheets of its control; nothing is cut from our sheet. Excluded, and only these: (1) glyph pixels within 2 px of text ink of the reference (ink = more than 60 levels from the local background, which is the commonest colour of the crop in a 25 x 13 window, so white text on a selection fill counts; runs of 13 or more, dotted focus lines, hollow frames and filled boxes are not ink; a piece counts as text when it is a word or has a neighbour in its line within 6 px; isolated check ticks, chevrons, radio dots, carets and selection fills are chrome and stay in); (2) v3 coloured artwork: in the toolbar, tab, combo list, check list, group box and button sheets low-contrast non-run pieces of 8 to 24 px grown to a 16 x 16 slot, and in the popup menu sheets whatever differs from the row background in the icon column x 3 to 27 (separators and row fills stay in); no rectangle outside those rules, none in the dark comparison. The rectangle lists are in the scratch output (`art` per crop; 29 slots in each toolbar crop, 10 in the stats tab strip, one per menu icon row); button_kinds crop 4 (the arrow picture button, the v3 up-arrow bitmap at 498,111, 12 x 14) is the one hand-set rectangle. Negative control: shifting ours by one pixel gives 215 to 7336 flat px off in every claimed sheet tested, so the metric is sensitive. Tolerance: 0 flat px is matched; up to 5 px per crop is accepted only where the pixels are shown to be glyph or icon edges (listed below).

Result. Windows (light): matched 20, review 9, started 1, ready 0 (workers claimed matched 21). Windows dark: matched 3, review 16, started 11 (workers claimed matched 11, review 8, started 11). Page: Windows Light 85 %, Dark 49 %, the Windows flavor 67 %, all five flavors 49 %.

Windows light, 275 sheets with a reference, 635 crops. Reproduced at 0 flat px off in every crop: label 12, button 84 (button_kinds arrow button excepted as artwork: 8 px in the v3 bitmap), check box 29, radio 20, radio group 17, edits (spin edit, mask edit, button edit, text edit) 37, token edit 3, image combo box 29, check list box 15, group box 4, page control 21, splitter 9, status bar 8, menu bar 21, hint 6, list box 3. Within glyph or icon edge noise: toolbar button 11 crops at 0 to 3 px (one icon edge column at x 768), popup menu 77 crops, 52 at 0 and the rest 1 to 5 px (the hyphen of the disabled "Num -" and icon corners, 115 px in all). Downgraded: scroll bar (matched to review): scroll_bar_native crop 3 finds no thumb at the native position (112 flat px at its best placement, a 2 x 36 thumb column and the arrow end), and the committed scroll_bar_states sheet is not what master renders. Unchanged: combo box review (6 crops 13 to 15 px: combo_box_open_size, list_size2, list_size5), text edit review (no TcxTextEdit capture), page control and group box hold. Largest remaining light gaps (flat px, one crop): data_grid_hover8 445, data_grid_group 378, data_grid_group3 361, tree_list_focus and tree_list_check 135, vertical_grid_states 43; the dialog sheets (dialog_more3 348,860) are whole dialogs without a reference per control.

Windows dark. Reproduced at 4 px or less: checkbox (15 of 15 at 0), scroll bar (14 of 14 at 4 px, the arrow glyph), mask edit (the single-line edit captures, all 0). Downgraded to review: label (the four syslink captures 8 to 10 px; rest and disabled 2 and 1), button (button-split-disabled 9 px, the arrow has #CCCCCC pixels ours lacks), text edit (seven single-line captures 0, but every multi-line capture 12 px and explorer 1003, vscroll 2375), spin edit (22 to 51 px, pressed worst), combo box (18 px in focus and open, 84 in combo-list-disabled), image combo box (shares the combo captures), popup menu (6 to 43 flat px in all six captures), hint (tooltip captures not placeable, 1862 and 1956 px). Not claimed and unchanged: radio 123 to 238 px, trackbar, progress bar, group box, tab, tree, status bar, list view, month calendar all far off.

Re-renders from master (working tree compile, sheet apps from scripts/looks_scale_sheets.json, `NEPER_LOOKS_EXACT` must be left unset: with it set even committed light sheets differ). The 10 random sheets (toolbar_button_hot, popup_menu_help, image_combo_box_open_auth2, combo_box_open_size, page_control_stats_5, popup_menu_sub_cards_print_parent, splitter_live_hover, popup_menu, image_combo_box_open_auth, page_control): light 1.0 equal 10 of 10; dark 1.0 equal 7 of 10, not equal image_combo_box_open_auth (3726 px), image_combo_box_open_auth2 (3901), combo_box_open_size (209). A clean `git archive` tree exhausted the compiler arena on three apps, so the working tree was compiled (its uncommitted changes are outside lib/e/ui). A full re-render of all 253 sheet entries at 1.0 and 1.5 (1012 files) found 911 equal: 40 dark sheets at 1.0 and 40 at 1.5 are stale (31 combo_box_*, 18 image_combo_box_* dialog, hover and open ones, tree_list_check, focus, hover, hover2, data_grid_bottom2 and 3), and light: scroll_bar_states (488 px at 1.0), 18 light sheets at 1.5 (splitter live, status_bar report and hover, toolbar_button_row, tree_list; data_grid_states and its pressed sheet are written by another argument list than the table says, their shape differs). The committed combo and image combo dark sheets therefore do not show master.

Scale (`looks_scale_check.py`, 6 sheets drawn at random): 5 of 5 evaluated pass at 1.25, 1.5 and 2.0 (image_combo_box_list_auth2 75.2 / 77.2 / 100, image_combo_box_list_sort 50.6 / 100 / 100 on the exact column, page_control_bottom_hover2 97.9, page_control_stats_10 99.4 / 98.6 / 92.2, popup_menu_sub_cards_print_child 100); the sixth, data_grid_shortcuts_states3, is not run by the script (0 of 0).

Caveats. My text mask is a heuristic (it also excludes a check tick when it has a neighbour in its line, and a 2 px halo round dense text such as the tab strip, 50 % of that crop); results can move by a few pixels with it. Dark flat is the stricter dilated rule, so dark matched needs the whole capture within 4 px.

# Looks audit 5, 2026-10-16 (Windows light and Windows dark only)

Independent recompute of the two Windows looks at HEAD dd6b28824 after rounds 11 and 12 (every sheet app freshly rendered and committed). Own tools (a mask-based comparer, a native-dark matcher and a manifest crediting script, kept in a scratch folder), not `scripts/looks_*.py`. The workers' tools were checked first: `looks_strict_dark.py` excludes from its flat count every pixel whose luminance is above 70 inside the text zone (`glyph_mask`), which removes a selection box (#0078D7 or #008CFB, luminance 95 to 111) from the comparison entirely; `looks_strict.py` and `looks_cells2.py` use the reference mask alone and are sound, but their text zone is a height and density filter that also swallows the frames of selected rows.

Result. Windows (light): matched 15, review 14, started 1, ready 0 (workers claimed matched 16, review 13, started 1; audit 4 had matched 13). Windows dark: matched 1, review 17, started 12 (workers claimed matched 5, review 13, started 12; audit 4 had matched 0). Page completion: Windows Light 79 %, Dark 45 %, the Windows flavor 62 %, all five flavors 48 %.

Downgrades against the workers' claims. Light: group box (matched to review: the three Options title bars fail the ink rule, the caption ends at 79 and 59 against 83 and 61 once the v3 icon from x 436 is masked, and sits 1 px low; the untouched Deck Legality frame passes). Dark: text edit, spin edit, combo box and mask edit (matched to review). The native selection fill is #0078D7; ours is #008CFB in the edit, spin edit and mask edit, so edit-selected has 76 flat px off (66 selection fill, 36 levels) and spin-focus 12; edit-focus has 15 px (caret 2 px right, 56 levels); combo-edit-open-hover-row has 121 flat px off (the native head text is selected, ours is not) and combo-edit-hover-arrow 156 (the capture's field is empty). The mask edit has no native capture and fails against edit-selected the same way (191 flat px). The dark check box stays matched. Kept as the workers set them: radio (the Search Cards hover grab differs from rest only by the caret column of the Search edit, verified on the grabs), image combo box, toolbar button and the other light matched controls.

## Method

The committed PNGs are what HEAD renders (the tree was just re-rendered; nothing was re-rendered here). Reference integrity: the 278 sheets still cut (XY cut, gap 6) into 644 crops; the two references changed since audit 4 are verified: button_arrows (new, four exact sub-images of the Card Art grabs) and group_box_dialog (the interior is no longer blanked: its four crops are exact sub-images of grabs). Comparison as in audit 4: structural pixel of the reference (run of 11 or more equal pixels, colour 30 or more times, not within 1 px of non-flat), off above 2 levels in any channel, reference mask alone; crops compared in place, or placed at the offset of fewest flat px off (ties broken by ink boxes). Text zone: ink pieces (more than 60 levels from the crop background, outside runs) with a bounding box of at most 18 x 30 and 150 px, grown by 2 px; a pixel of at most 4 levels within 3 px of ink is a glyph edge. Excluded: v3 toolbar icon bitmaps (groups up to 20 x 20, and the centre of a hot face, in both images), the card combo icons from x 140, the group box icon from x 436, the menu bar hot_view reference beyond x 768 (the grab stops inside Reports; the rest is unverified). Ink boxes at 60, 100 and 150 levels within 1 px (60 and one of the others, or 100 and 150 when lines exist), plus a luminance correlation check on short lines. Manifest rows were credited by an exact sub-image search of the crops in the 502 grabs; rows whose crop is a composite or an edit are not found by it, so for controls whose sheets and references are byte for byte audit 4's (label, check box, radio group, spin edit, mask edit, button edit, token edit, list box, splitter, status bar, toolbar button, menu bar, hint) its state credits stand.

Windows dark. Reference = native dark @1x. Each capture's whole control rect is matched in our native_dark sheets at a free offset (at most 3 px around the best match) and compared with the 8-neighbour flat rule and the text zone above; nothing is cut. Light sizes are unchanged: every control audit 4 matched still passes its crops.

Scale. `looks_scale_check.py` on the 12 sheets: 12 of 12 pass at 1.5 and at 2.0, numbers as in audit 4 (button 94.2 and 98.3, spin edit 96.6 and 99.2, status bar 96.4 and 95.6, image combo box 98.7 and 99.9, the rest 100). The own stricter run check was not repeated.

# Looks audit 4, 2026-10-16 (Windows light and Windows dark only)

Independent recompute of the two Windows looks after rounds 4 to 11 (30 controls x 2). Nothing here trusts a self-report. Scale 1.0, PIL and numpy, `python -I`, scratch tools in a temp folder (not the workers' `scripts/looks_*.py`). Audit 3 and its evidence check of the other looks follow below, unchanged.

Result. Windows (light): matched 13, review 16, started 1, ready 0 (workers claimed matched 15, review 14, started 1; audit 3 had matched 7, review 19, started 4). Windows dark: matched 0, review 17, started 13 (workers claimed matched 3, review 16, started 11). Page completion after this audit: Windows Light 76 %, Dark 43 %, the Windows flavor 59 %, all five flavors 47 %.

Windows (light) changes against the workers' claims. Downgraded from matched: combo box (one flat pixel 3 levels off in the flat card combo, one 4 levels off in the open Condition list head, the in-place grid editor combo captured with no cell), image combo box (the same head pixel; the open Art 2 reference head shows another item than ours), group box (the open frame reference has its interior blanked, the three Options title bars fail the ink rule, the page header hover grab has no cell). Upgraded from the workers' review: list box. Windows dark: the three matched claims (text edit, spin edit, mask edit) fall to review: the native dark edit is 150 x 24 and ours 141 x 22, and the workers' zero followed cutting two plain rows (against the uncut capture 693 of 3137 flat px are off at rest). Toolbar button and vertical grid dark fall from review to started (no native dark reference exists for either).

## Method

Fresh renders. All 131 `examples/looks` apps were compiled with the committed `build/windows/neper-self.exe` (arena 4g) and run in 361 (app, argument) combinations in a scratch folder (every no-argument app run, the argument lists of `scripts/looks_scale_sheets.json`, the 892 candidate lists of `looks_scale_check.py` for the 12 multi-sheet apps, `page_control t12 t13 t14`). Every Windows and Windows dark PNG a run wrote was hashed against the committed one; no sheet had two writers with different bytes. Light: of 616 committed PNGs, 412 are byte for byte what HEAD renders; at scale 1.0 that is 303 of 308 (not reproduced: dialog_more, dialog_more2 and dialog_more3, last committed at 85c946922 before later lib changes, and the two scale tile sheets), and 199 of 308 `@1.5x` PNGs differ from a fresh render. Dark: 331 of 618 identical; 87 of 309 at 1.0 differ (every popup_menu_sel_*, popup_menu_sub_* and menu_bar_open_* sheet, data_grid_bottom2 and bottom3, data_grid_states, dialog_more*, list_box_blur, list_box_dialog, list_box_focus) and 200 at 1.5. All comparisons below use the fresh renders. One app does not run at HEAD: `toolbar_button toolbar_button_hot` traps (`build_hot` returns 10 cells for 9 allocated, `slice end 10 out of bounds for len 9`); a scratch copy with `ret (9usize, ok)` reproduces the committed toolbar_button_hot.png byte for byte, so only the app source is wrong. `linux_button` also exits 1 (not a Windows sheet).

Reference integrity. The 278 `ref/windows` sheets split into 644 crops (XY cut on the sheet background). Hashed and pixel-checked against the 606 raw grabs: 618 are exact sub-images of one grab, 11 are exact except their last row (the three toolbar sheets: row 25 from x 288 on is the bar's own #D2D2D2 edge, edited in the reference, not captured), 15 are composites or edited (6 combo and 6 image combo open lists = a head crop over a popup crop, the group box frame with its interior blanked, the radio dialog group, the older 150 % data_grid@1.5x capture). Six reference sheets have no sheet of ours: combo_box_flat-left, data_grid_missing, data_grid_sort, scroll_bar_live-across, splitter_live-across and the raw main-window grab. The token edit references are real exact crops (audit 3's stand-in finding does not hold).

Comparison. Structural pixel of the reference: a pixel in a straight run of 11 or more equal pixels whose colour occurs 30 or more times in the sheet and that is not within 1 px of a non-flat pixel; off = more than 2 levels in any channel. The mask comes from the reference only. The workers' `looks_cells.py` ANDs it with the structural mask of ours, so a reference-flat pixel where ours is not flat (an anti-aliased corner) is never counted; two such pixels exist (combo_box_flat_card 1 px at 3 levels, image_combo_box_open_condition_head 1 px at 4 levels) and are counted here. Same-size sheets are compared in place (ours at the reference position); where the sheet sizes differ the crop is placed at the offset of fewest flat-px off within the sheet (the offset is a free parameter, only sizes and inner geometry are tested). Excluded and counted apart: pixels within 2 px of non-flat text ink (glyph anti-aliasing against ClearType, reported as `text`), v3's coloured and gray icon bitmaps (toolbar strips: every group of pixels up to 20 x 20 that differs from the strip background; the combo flat card crops: the neighbouring toolbar icons from x 156; the group box title bars: the icon area from x 436), and the edited toolbar row is compared as given. Ink boxes: lines of pixels more than 60, 100 or 150 levels from the cell background, rows within 2 blank rows count as one line (a mnemonic underline), boxes within 1 px at 60 and at 100 or 150. A crop passes with 0 chrome px off and its ink boxes in agreement; a text line whose glyphs differ in content (a second check on luminance after the best 1 px shift) also fails it.

States. The 558 manifest rows of `ref/windows/states` (a row is a captured state; the 49 dialog and main-window rows are bases). A row is credited to a crop when the crop is an exact sub-image of that grab and the grab differs from its nearest same-size rest grab inside the crop; `identical` = the grab equals its rest grab (no distinct look) or the pressed grab is unchanged inside the held crop built from it. `matched` needs every captured state of the control credited and passing, so a state with no crop keeps a control at review; uncaptured states are listed as unverified; nothing is `ready`.

Windows dark. Reference = `ref/windows-native/dark` at @1x (191 captures). `matched` needs the native dark geometry, text and every native-dark-captured state; the native-light-in-dark controls (tab control, status bar, date picker and calendar, menu bar, message box and task dialog, group box frame, progress bar, list box and combo drop list, the light toolbar) have no dark reference and stay `started` where nothing was compared. Numbers come from placing the native control rect in our dark sheet at the offset of fewest flat-px off, without cutting rows.

## Scale robustness (sample of 12 sheets)

The workers' `scripts/looks_scale_check.py` on button, checkbox_states, radio_group_states, edits_dialog, spin_edit, combo_box, image_combo_box, popup_menu_file, tree_list, data_grid_top, status_bar and menu_bar reports every sheet passing at 1.5 and at 2.0 (exact / within a device pixel, thresholds 93 and 95: button 94.2 / 100 and 98.3 / 100, spin edit 96.6 / 99.4 and 99.2 / 99.4, status bar 96.4 / 100 and 95.6 / 100, image combo box 98.7 / 100 and 99.9 / 100, the rest 100). Its checker leaves text out, lets a run end softly where the 1.0 edge is a blend and rounds like `winpx.edge` (the rule under test). An own check (text left out like theirs, every 1.0 run of 8 or more equal pixels outside the sheet background must be a run of scale times the length at the rounded place, colour within 8 levels, the pixel just past each end not of the run colour; exact / within 1 px) gives, at 1.5 and 2.0: button 90.5 / 94.3 and 98.5 / 99.7; check box 100 and 100; radio group 100 and 100; edits_dialog 100 and 100; spin edit 89.4 / 92.5 and 85.5 / 92.8; combo box 100 and 100; image combo box 86.1 / 95.8 and 83.7 / 92.8; popup menu file 100 and 100; tree list 92.5 / 100 and 100; data grid top 99.7 / 99.9 and 99.7 / 99.9; status bar 91.6 / 99.2 and 90.4 / 98.4; menu bar 100 and 100. The runs that fail on the image combo box are the vector stand-in icons (a 2.0 render viewed shows them crisp); spin edit and image combo box at 2.0 are just under the 93 % threshold on the stricter check. Scale stays informational for the statuses.

## Findings for the maintainers

- `scripts/looks_cells.py` ANDs the reference's structural mask with that of ours (`smask & omask`), which hides a reference-flat pixel where ours is not flat; the two pixels above were hidden that way. Its ink rule groups rows without merging a mnemonic underline, which makes the 'Row Color:' label crop fail at the 60 level (it passes at 100 and 150).
- 204 light and 287 dark committed PNGs are not what HEAD renders (87 dark and 5 light sheets at 1.0, 199 and 200 at 1.5); the page thumbnails of the dark popup, menu bar and grid sheets are stale. `toolbar_button_hot` traps at HEAD (`build_hot` returns 10 for 9 cells).
- Reference edits that are not live pixels: the toolbar crops' row 25 from x 288, the blanked Deck Legality interior, the head over popup composites of the 12 open list sheets.
- The scale checker's tolerances (soft ends, text left out, colour 8, runs within 1) are looser than an exact run test; see the sample above.

# Looks audit 3, 2026-10-16

Independent recompute after rounds 4 to 7 (226 interaction-state captures, 298 + 34 hover captures, winpx scale robustness, dark variants of all four flavors). Nothing here trusts a self-report. A fresh render check first: 16 apps (button, checkbox, radio, mask_edit, spin_edit, button_edit, text_edit, popup_menu, data_grid, edits_*, button_*, checkbox_states, radio_states) were rebuilt into a scratch folder and their 240 Neper, Windows, Windows dark and Neper dark PNGs at 1.0 and 1.5 are byte for byte the committed ones, so the committed sheets are current.

## Method (Windows light)

Every ref sheet in `ref/windows/` (230 PNGs, 711 crops: the connected non-background regions) was matched against our sheet with PIL and numpy, `python -I`, scale 1.0. Where the ref sheet has the size of ours the crop is compared in place (cells are on the same grid); where the ref is a single crop and ours a full sheet the crop is placed at the offset of least structural error within 4 px (the offset is then a free parameter, only sizes and inner geometry are tested). 693 of 711 crops were found as exact sub-images of one of the 606 raw captures (558 state grabs plus dialog, menu and main-window grabs; hash then pixel check); of the other 18, 15 are composites (6 combo and 6 image combo open lists, the Select Format frame with its interior blanked, the radio dialog group, the main-window data grid) and 3 are tiny exact crops (two 9x5 menu glyphs and a 1 px splitter strip). Each captured state (558 manifest rows) was credited to a cell only when the state differs from its nearest rest grab inside the crop.

Strict rule as in audit 2: structural = a ref pixel in a flat fill or a straight run of 11 or more equal pixels (colour seen 30 times or more, not within 1 px of ink); tolerance 2 levels per channel; text anti-aliasing and ClearType excluded. Added: text ink boxes (pixels more than 60, 100 and 150 levels from the crop background, lines grouped by rows) must agree within 1 px at the 60 level and at one of the other two; v3 coloured icon and card artwork is excluded. A cell passes when it has 0 structural pixels off and its ink boxes agree. `matched` needs every captured state of the control to have a cell and every own cell to pass; states with no capture are listed as unverified; `ready` also needs every state the control can have to be captured and matching.

Result: matched 7, ready 0, review 19, started 4. No control can be `ready`: no control has a capture of its disabled state (the single disabled capture, the Edit Rule button, has no cell), and spin edit, check box, radio group, mask edit, group box, label and status bar, the best cases, all lack disabled and one or more of indeterminate, read-only, invalid.

Hover: 148 cells sit in the hover sheets (button_hover, checkbox_hover, radio_hover, check_list_box_hover, combo_hover, edits_hover, tree_list_hover*, vertical_grid_hover, data_grid_*hover*, toolbar_button_hot, menu_bar_hot, status_bar_hover, title_bar_hover, splitter hover); all 148 are exact crops that also occur in a `hover-*` grab, so the claimed hover cells do use the real hover captures. 55 of the 148 pass. 7 of those cells come from grabs pixel-identical to their rest grab (check list rows, the Internet Authentication combo edit and the grouped grid rest): the control shows no distinct hover there. The four `*_held` sheets are pressed states from non-hover grabs.

Scale (informational for `matched`, none downgraded for it): `scripts/looks_scale_check.py` at 2.0 passes every baked-tile control except mask_edit (92.0 % of runs hold, threshold 93, clean on view): button 99.7, checkbox 96.9, radio 100, spin 94.7, button edit 96.7, text edit 93.2, popup menu file 100, data grid 99.6. At 1.5 (informational) button 76.4, checkbox 89.0, radio 87.2, mask edit 92.8, popup menu file 79.2 (uneven rings, dotted focus rectangles) while spin, button edit, text edit and data grid stay at 93 to 98. 58 Windows sheet apps were rendered at 2.0 and all ran except edits_hover, which dies with `e.mem.Exhausted` at 2.0 (the 4g arena). Viewed at 2.0: button, mask edit, page control (tab bevels double up) and check list box (blocky check glyphs).

Neper (light) is left at `ready` (the baseline: the look is its own reference); the contrast pass nevertheless flags two Neper sheets: label (3.8:1 text) and image combo box (3.2:1 text). Neper dark: `button`, `hint` fall to started and `toolbar_button` stays started because the PNG shows light text on the light accent face (1.1:1) although the earlier note measured the label at 7.7:1.

Unverified: caption pressed (not capturable), resize, disabled; inactive caption only for the main window |

Counts: matched 7, ready 0, review 19, started 4 (self-reported before the audit: matched 17, review 10, started 3).

Downgraded from self-reported matched to review: radio, text edit, combo box, image combo box, check list box, splitter, toolbar button, menu bar, popup menu, list box. Token edit fell from review to started (its reference sheet is a tab-control crop used as a stand-in, 1748 px off). Confirmed matched: label, check box, radio group, spin edit, mask edit, group box, status bar.

Ten largest remaining Windows mismatches (structural px off in one cell, tolerance 2 levels; dialog bodies are app content and left out): data_grid_bottom 9509; data_grid_states 9010; data_grid_main 8487; vertical_grid_states 2280; token_edit_dialog 1748 (stand-in reference); tree_list_rules 1462; check_list_box_hover 956; scroll_bar_live 938; toolbar_button_hot 469; button_hover 372.

## The other looks (evidence check)

Every one of the 30 controls has a PNG for each of the seven looks and every PNG referenced by `status.json` and the overlays resolves to an existing file; none is a single colour. For `review` the sheet must satisfy the measured floor on every component (text and its states 4.5:1 or better, other UI parts 3:1; a component below 4.5:1 is allowed only when it is equally low in the twin theme and 2:1 or better, which is how disabled and secondary text show up). The dark looks must also differ from the light twin (mean difference of at least 30 levels; all measure 149 to 229), show no light-only chrome (a white face on a dark page) and no word that passes in the light twin but falls under 4.5:1 in the dark one. `matched` is never used: none of these looks has a live reference.
